﻿coṟpoḻivu 2- (16-2-1965)

muṉṉurai

ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil tamiḻnāṭu irunta nilaiyiṉaiyum ataṉ cūḻnilaiyum oruvāṟu mutaṟ coṟpoḻivil kaṇṭōm. antac cūḻalkaḷukku iṭaiyil nāṭṭu moḻiyākiya tamiḻ palvēṟu vakaikaḷil vaḷarcciyaṭaiyattakka vāyppiṉaip peṟṟa nilaiyiṉaiyum ōraḷavu teḷintōm. iṉṟu atē nūṟṟāṇṭil tamiḻ urainaṭai vaḷarnta vakaikaḷai ārāytal poruttamuṭaittu eṉa eṇṇukiṟēṉ.

potu makkaḷ ilakkiyam

ilakkiya amaippil vaḷarvaṉa maṭṭumiṉṟi, potu makkaḷ vāḻvai viḷakkuvaṉavum vāḻvil iṭam peṟuvaṉavum urainaṭaiyiṉ pāl cārtti eṇṇattakkaṉavē eṉṟu ōraḷavu kaṇṭōm. eṉavē iṟaiyaṉār kaḷaviyalurai pōṉṟum, civañāṉa pāṭiyum pōṉṟum, peruṅkāppiyaṅkaḷiṉ urainaṭai pōṉṟum uḷḷaṉa. orupuṟam ilakkiya urai naṭaiyeṉa tōṉṟi vīṟunaṭaiyiṭṭu vaḷarntu tamiḻnāṭṭil vāḻntuvanta atē vēḷaiyil, kallā makkaḷiṭattum naṉku eḻuta paṭikkat teriyāta pāmara makkaḷiṭattum tattam karuttu parimāṟṟattukkākavum potumakkaḷ nalaṉukkākavum, aracāṅkattiṟkum makkaḷukkum uḷḷa toṭarpai vaḷarppataṟkākavum, palvēṟu nāṭṭu nalaṉkaḷukkākavum vāḻntu vaḷarntu vanta urainaṭaiyaiyum nām eṇṇip pārkka kaṭamaippaṭṭuḷḷōm. ivai pērilakkiyaṅkaḷeṉak kālattai veṉṟu vāḻat takkaṉa eṉak kāṇa muṭiyāviṭṭālum, ivaitām avvakkālaṅkaḷil vāḻnta makkaḷiṉ uṇmaiyāṉa vāḻkkai muṟai, paṇpāṭu, nākarikam iṉṉapiṟavaṟṟai ulakukku uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē, ivaṟṟaip ‘potumakkaḷ urainaṭai’ allatu 'potumakkaḷ ilakkiyam’ eṉa pakuttuk kāṇal cālapporuttamāṉatākum. makkaḷ vāḻkkai muṟaiyil ettaṉai ettaṉai pirivukaḷuṇṭō-avartam cūḻal ettaṉaik kettaṉai māṟupaṭukiṉṟatō-attaṉaikkattaṉai vakaiyil avartam pēccum vaḻakkum-cātāraṇa eḻuttu vaḻakkum māṟṟam peṟukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟai āṅkila muṟaippaṭi ‘koccai moḻi’ eṉṟālum ammoḻiyē nāṭṭup peṟumpāṉmaiyāṉa makkaḷiṉ vāḻvaip paṭampiṭittuk kāṭṭukiṉṟamaiyiṉ atu paṟṟiya āyvu iṉṟiyamaiyātatākiṉṟatu. iṉṟu moḻittuṟaiyai ārāykiṉṟa ulaka aṟiñarkaḷ palarum ittakaiya makkaḷ moḻiyākiya koccaimoḻiyai ārāyntu uṇarttum neṟiyilēyē pāṭupaṭukiṉṟaṉar eṉpatum kaṇkūṭu.eṉavē iṉṟu nām potumakkaḷiṭaiyē karuttup parimāṟṟattukkākavum: viḷakkam, viḷamparam mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkākavum, vēṭikkai poḻutupōkku mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkākavum vaḻaṅkap peṟṟa urainaṭai vakaikaḷaiyum katai, nāval. nāṭakam mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum ārāyalām eṉak karutukiṟēṉ.

mekkaṉci tuṟaiyiṉ paṇi

potu makkaḷōṭu toṭarpu koṇṭu avar tam moḻi nalattaiyum piṟa vāḻviyalpukaḷaiyum ārāynta aṟiñar palar ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil vāḻntiruntaṉar. pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭilum ataṟku muṉṉum vāḻnta makkaḷ vāḻkkaiyai ārāynta atē vēḷaiyil avar tam ilakkiya vaḷaṉaiyum vaḻakku moḻiyaiyum ārāynta mēlai nāṭṭavar nāṭṭil palar iruntaṉar. avaruḷ patiṉeṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyilum pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattilum vāḻntu, ciṟanta paṇiyāṟṟi tikaḻnta-tam ūtiyap paṇiyaiyum tamiḻ uṇarvup paṇiyaiyum tiṟampaṭac ceyta-lepṭiṉaṇṭ karṉal kāliṉ mekkaṉci (Lient. Col.Colin Mackenzie) avarkaḷ ciṟantavar eṉpatu maṟakka muṭiyāta uṇmai. araciyal tuṟaip paṇiyāḷarāka āṅkila nāṭṭiliruntu tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟku 1782-il vanta avar, tam paṇiyōṭu paṇiyākac cellumiṭaṅkaḷilellām vāḻnta kallā makkaḷōṭu kalantu uṟavāṭi, avar tam koccai moḻiyaiyum vaḷamuṟṟa ilakkiya vaḷaṉaiyum ārāyntu eḻuti vaittuḷḷār. muppatteṭṭu āṇṭukaḷ (1783—1821) palvēṟu māvaṭṭaṅkaḷilum cuṟṟip paṇiyāṟṟiya nāṭkaḷil avar tokuttut tantaṉa pala. avar tam aluval nilai uyara uyara-avar tam āṭcip poṟuppiṉ ellai viriya viriya. avartam moḻiyāyvum virintukoṇṭē vantatu. tamiḻ nāṭṭil maṭṭumiṉṟi ilaṅkai, jāvā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilum paṇiyāṟṟiya avar iṟutiyil intiyap peru nilapparappiṟkē, oru tuṟaikku-aḷavai āyvuttuṟaikku- (Surveyor General of India) talaivarāṉār. eṉavē avar ceyalakam kalkattāviṟku māṟṟappaṭṭatu. eṉiṉum, avartam tamiḻmoḻi āyvu muṟṟuppeṟātu, avaratu 68 vatu vayatu varai, avar iṟuti nāḷ varai (1821) vaḷarntu koṇṭē tāṉ iruntatu. avartam āyvukku utaviyavaruḷ ‘karṉal vilks’ (Col.Wilks) eṉṟa aṟiñarum oruvarāvar. avartam āyvukaḷ pala āṅkila nāṭṭiṟku aṉuppap peṟṟaṉa. avaṟṟai veḷiyiṭa akkālak ‘kavarṉar jeṉaralāṉa’ vāraṉ hēsṭṭiṅ avarkaḷāl uttaravum tarappeṟṟatu. avartam attakaiya tamiḻ, teluṅku mutaliya ēṭṭup piratikaḷaic cērkkap patiṉaiyāyiram pavuṇṭu (E15,000/–) celavāyiṟṟeṉavum kaṇakkiṭappeṟṟatu. avartam maṟaivukkup piṟaku 1828-il pōrāciriyar vilcaṉ (Prof. Wilson) avarkaḷ tāmē valiya vantu attokuppai veḷiyiṭṭār anta veḷiyīṭṭil perumpāṉmai tamiḻilēyē uḷḷaṉa. oru cila teluṅkum kaṉṉaṭamum iṭam peṟa amaikiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ cila āṅkila nāṭṭu ‘irāyal ēciyāṭṭik cocaiṭṭi’ (Royal Asiatic Society) yiṉarāl peyarkkap peṟṟu veḷiyiṭavum peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ittokuppaip paṟṟi ‘mārk vilks’ eṉpār.

﻿The collection gives all that is necessary to illustrate the antiquities, the civil, military and religious institutions and ancient History of South India.





(Col. Mark Wilks-History of Mysore)

eṉṟu pārāṭṭiyuḷḷār. revareṇṭu villiyam ṭeylar (Rev. William Taylor) mutaliya palar avaṟṟai ārāyntu aṭikkaṭi āyvuk kaṭṭuraikaḷum āyvu nūlkaḷum veḷiyiṭṭaṉar. avaṟṟuḷ 1837-38 il cila tokukkap peṟṟaṉa. piṟaku 1858il ‘kiḻakkintiya maṉai’yum (EastIndia House) piravucu maṉuskiripṭcuṭaṉ (BrownesManuscripts) toṭarpu koḷḷa inta 'mekkaṉci cērkkai' (Collection) veḷivantatu. ceṉṉai aracāṅka uṭaimaiyāka (1847) āṉa piṟaku, 1882-l ittokuti kīḻtticaik kaiyēṭṭup piratit tokuppil iṭam peṟṟatu. akkālattil atil 8,000 vakaikaḷ iruntaṉavām. mekkaṉci avarkaḷē ittokuppaik koṇṭu varattāmpaṭṭa pāṭṭaik kūṟuvataik kāṇalām.

“From 1792 to 1799 it were tedious to relate the difficulties, the accidents and the discouragements that impeded the progress of this design. The slender means allottted from the necessity of a rigid, no doubt just, economy, the doubts and hindrance ever attendant on new attempts; difficulties arising from the nature of the climate of the country, of the Government, from conflecting interest and passions and prejudices, difficult to contend with, and unpleasant to recollect.”





“In the whole of this period, in which I have marched or wandered, ower the most of the provinces south of the ‘Krishna’, I look back with regret to objects now known to exist, that could have been then examined, and to trace of customs and of institutions that could have been explained, had time or means admitted of the enquiry.



“It was only after my expedition to Ceylon in 1796, that accident rather than design, though ever searching for light that were denied to my situation, threw in any way, those means that I have since unceasingly pursued (not without some Success, I hope) of penetrating beyond the common surface of the Antiquities, the History and the Institutions of the South of India.”



“How for the idea suggested was fulfilled it is not for me to say; for adverse cireumstance one part was nearly defeated;and the ‘Natural History’ was never analyzed in the manner I proposed and expected in concert with the survey; the suspense I was placed in from the reduction of the slender stipend allotted to myself, both for salary and to provide for increasing contingencies, was not only sufficiently modifying, but was aggravated by the overthrow of the establishment first arranged for the work, while other branches were favoured, in the application of the Director’s order. The efforts of these measures on the public mind and even on my assistants contributed to paralise every effort for its completion, but not withstanding these difficulties the success attending the first researches, and a convicition of its utility, induced me to persevere till 1808.”





﻿ ivvāṟu avarē ittuṟaiyil tām mēṟkoṇṭa muyaṟciyaiyum ataṟku uṇṭāṉa palvēṟu iṭaiyūṟukaḷaiyum viḷakkik kāṭṭukiṉṟar. avartam veḷiyīṭṭut tokuppil tamiḻil 192[1] kaiyēṭṭuppaṭikaḷ appōtu tokukkap peṟṟaṉa. purāṇam, kaṭṭukkatai, uḷnāṭṭu varalāṟu, vāḻkkai varalāṟu, katai, nāṭakam, tattuvam, vāṉa nūl, maruttuvam, kalai mutaliya palvēṟu pirivukaḷum itil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivaiyaṉṟi ovvōr ūrilum vaḻaṅkum ciṟukataikaḷ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ paṟṟiyum (local tracts) tamiḻil 255 tokukkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivvāṟu avar tam kālattil tamiḻ nāṭṭu mūlai muṭukkukaḷil vaḻaṅkappeṟṟuḷḷa palvēṟu vakai vāḻvu muṟaikaḷ paṟṟiya kuṟippukaḷ aṉaittaiyum tāmē nēril ceṉṟu kaṇṭu, kēṭṭu, eḻutit taracceytu veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār. avaṟṟuḷ oru cila nām kāṇa irukkiṟōm. avai aṉaittum (mikac cila tavirttu) urainaṭaiyil uḷḷamaiyiṉ namatu āyviṭaiyil iṭam peṟukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟāl nām veṟum uraiṭaiyaip paṟṟi maṭṭum aṟiyavillai. attuṭaṉ akkālap paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷaiyum moḻi amaippu, piṟamoḻik kalappu, koccai vaḻakkiṉ aḷavu mutaliya palavaṟṟaiyum aṟintu koḷḷa muṭikiṉṟatu.

jāṉ morṭāk paṇi

ittuṟaiyil ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭu pāṭupaṭṭa aṟiñarkaḷ palar eṉṟāēm. avarkaḷ aṉaivaraippaṟṟiyum eṇṇavum eḻutavum namakku nēramum iṭamum illai. eṉavē avaruḷ ciṟantavarākiya mekkaṉci paṟṟiyē kuṟippiṭṭēṉ. avartam tokuppaṉaittum pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil nāṭṭilvāḻntaṉa paṟṟiyaṉavēyām. itē tuṟaiyil ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṭaikālattil ayarātu uḻaitta maṟṟeruvarai iṅkē eṇṇip pārttal cālum eṉak karutukiṟēṉ. avarē jāṉ mor ṭāk (John Mordoch) eṉpavar. iṟṟaikkuccariyāka oru nūṟṟāṇṭukku muṉ, 1865-il, nāṭṭil tamiḻmoḻiyil accēṟi veḷivanta nūlkaḷaiyellām tokuttu, attokutikkut teḷinta tēvaiyāṉa oru muṉṉuraiyaiyum eḻuti avar veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār. eṉavē, ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṭaikkālattil eḻutappeṟṟa tamiḻ nūl paṟṟiya viḷakkam tarum oru nalla viḷakkāka ivar tam tokuppu amaikiṉṟatu. 'kiṟittavak kīḻtticaik kalvik kuḻu’ eṉṟa amaippiṉ muyaṟciyāl ceṉṉaiyil ivartam nūl 1865-il veḷiyāyiṟṟu. itil pala tuṟaikaḷum naṉku pakukkap peṟṟu ovvoṉṟilum veḷiyāṉa nūlkaḷaip paṟṟiya kuṟippukkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. mutalil ‘camayam’ eṉṟa talaippilum, piṉ piṟa talaippukaḷilum, avai uḷḷaṉa. 1755 nūlkaḷ ittokuppil uḷḷaṉa.

innūlkaḷ paṟṟi jāṉ morṭāk avarkaḷ tokuttuk kāṇa virumpiya vakaiyiṉai avar vākkilēyē tarukiṟēṉ. The following might each form the subject of a volume.



1.﻿ The rise of the Saiva system in South India, notices of the Principal Saiva temples, a full list of Saiva books, with summaries of their content and some illustrative extracts.



2.﻿ A corresponding work on the Vaishnava system.



3.﻿ An account of the works on Philosophical Hinduism which analyses of their contents and copious extracts.



4.﻿ A complete account of the work on Astrology, Divination and Magic.



5.﻿ An account of Tamil Medicine.



6.﻿ A work on Tamil poetry specimens of versification should be given (Translation like Kambar). -



A book on the above subjects, will got up, would meet with remunerative sales in India, Europe and America and gain some name for the author.





ivvāṟu 1865 il ivartam muṉṉuraiyil tamatu ācaiyai veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār. eṉiṉum - cariyāka oru nūṟṟaṇṭu kaḻintum - urimai peṟṟu moḻi vaḷarkkiṉṟāēm eṉṟu kūṟikkoḷḷum iṉṟu varaiyum-ivar kāṭṭiya vakaiyil ceyalāṟṟuvōrai nāṭṭil iṉṉum kāṇa iyalavillai. ivartam muyaṟciyāl kāṇak kūṭiya vakaiyil uḷḷa nūlkaḷuḷ perumpālāṉa urainaṭai nūlkaḷē. eṉavē ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ urainaṭaiyaik kāṇa ivar tam tokuppum naṉku payaṉpaṭat takkatākum.

aracāṅka nūṟpativuc caṭṭam

1867kkup piṟaku aṉṟaiya aracāṅkattār accuc caṭṭa aṭippaṭaiyil, nāṭṭil accākum nūlkaḷil ovvoṉṟiṉ paṭiyum aracāṅkattiṟku (Registrar of Books) aṉuppivaikka vēṇṭumeṉa āṇaiyiṭṭaṉar. eṉavē ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil veḷiyāṉa nūlkaḷ aṉaittum aracāṅka muttiraiyaip peṟṟu, varicaippaṭuttap peṟṟu vakaiyāka eṇṇiṭap peṟṟuc cērttuvaikkap peṟṟaṉa. aṇmaiyil tamiḻ nāṭṭu aracāṅkattār avaṟṟait (1867–1900) tokuttu mūṉṟu pakutikaḷāka veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷaṉar. attokuppilum pala urainaṭai nūlkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. eṉavē ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil vaḷarnta tamiḻ urainaṭai vaḷarcciyai iṭaiyīṭiṉṟi ārāya ivaiyum ivai pōṉṟa vēṟu cilavum namakku ātāramāka uḷḷaṉa.[2] avaṟṟiṉ tuṇai koṇṭu iṉṟu "potu makkaḷ ilakkiya" urainaṭai paṟṟi ārāyntu eṭuttuk kāṭṭukaḷuṭaṉ viḷakkalām eṉa eṇṇukiṟēṉ.

veṟṟiyum koṭaiyum

veḷiyāṉa nūlkaḷum ēṭṭup piratiyil uḷḷaṉa pōka nāṭṭil aṅkaṅkē vaḻakkattil uḷḷa palvēṟu eḻuttukkaḷaiyum eṇṇutal poruntum. aracāṅkattārum piṟarum vaṭitta kalveṭṭukkaḷum kaṭanta nūṟṟāṇṭil cila uḷḷaṉa. veṟṟi kuṟittum, vēṟu pālam mutaliyaṉa amaittum uṇṭākkiya cācaṉaṅkaḷ nāṭṭil uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟil ōriraṇṭiṉaik īṇṭuk kāṭṭal nalam payappatākum.

ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil neppōliyaṉ vīḻcciyuṟṟatum āṅkilēyar veṟṟi peṟṟatum nāṭaṟinta varalāṟu. avveṟṟiyaik koṇṭāṭu mukattāṉ āṅkilēyar tam āṇai ceṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷilellām veṟṟittūṇ nāṭṭi, avveṟṟiyaik kallilum poṟittaṉar. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu tañcaic carapōci maṉṉaṉ āṭci ellaikku uṭpaṭṭa cāḷuvaṉkuppattil uḷḷatai iṉṟum kāṇalām. āṅkilattilum tamiḻilum akkuṟippukkaḷ amaintuḷḷaṉa. tamiḻkkuṟippu urai naṭaiyākavē uḷḷatu. iraṇṭaiyum appaṭiyē tarukiṟēṉ.

āṅkilēyar kalvi

1858-kkumuṉ kumpiṉi āṭci naṭaipeṟṟatu. ataṟkup piṟaku āṅkilēyar nāṭṭu nēraṭi āṭciyākiya vikṭōriyā irāṇiyiṉ āṭci toṭarntatu. akkālaṅkaḷilellām āṅkilēyar palar vāṇipam poruṭṭum āḷutaṟ poruṭṭum innāṭṭukku vantaṉar. vantavar innāṭṭu moḻiyaik kaṟṟaṉar. avarkaḷ kaṟṟa nūlkaḷum pala; kaṟpittōrum palar. avarkaḷaip paṟṟiya aṉṟaiya kuṟippoṉṟaik kāṇalām.

tamiḻ ilakkaṇat teḷivu ⁠⁠1893

aṅkilēyar viyāpāra māḷikaikaḷaic ceṉṉai, kaṭalūr mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil kaṭṭi viyāpārattaip perukki, aracum kaipaṟṟukiṉṟa camayattil, aṅkila uttiyōkastarkaḷ tamiḻaik kaṟpatu yuktameṉa atikārikaḷ tīrmāṉittu, ceṉṉaiyil ‘kālēj’ eṉṟa ōr





vittiyācālaiyai stāpittārkaḷ. itutāṉ ‘Old College’ eṉappaṭṭa iṭam. itil ippōtu vittiyācālait talaivarākiya ‘Director’ tamatu kaccēriyai vaittu naṭatti varukiṟār. itil mutal aṅkilēyarukkut tamiḻ upāttiyāyarāka niyamikkappaṭṭavar ceṅkaṟpaṭṭut tāṇṭavarāya mutaliyār. ivar akkālattil paṭṭaṇattil vijayam ceytu, naṉṉūlukkuk kāṇṭikai urai yiyaṟṟiṉa irāmāṉujak kavirāyariṉ cīṣaṉum, kaṟṟuc colliyumāyiruntu, mēṟpaṭi kavirāyariṭam naṉṉūṟpāṭam kēṭṭavar. ivar mūlamāyttāṉ aṅkilēyar naṉṉūl ciṟanta tamiḻ ilakkaṇam eṉat terintu koṇṭatu maṉṟi, carvakalācālaip pariṣaikaṭkum (University Examinations) in naṉṉūlē nalla ilakkaṇam eṉa vitikkavum talaippaṭṭatu.



it tāṇṭavarāya mutaliyārē vācaka pustakam (Prose) aṅkilēya māṇākkarukku vēṇṭiyatāyirunta poḻutu, hitōpatēcattaic camaskirutattiṉiṉṟu moḻip peyarttup pañca tantiram eṉṟa pustakam uṇṭu paṇṇik koṭuttavar. naṉṉūliluḷḷa puṇarcci vitikaḷ pulappaṭattakkatāy appustakattaik koṭum puṇarppā yeḻutiviṭṭār. ivar ceṅkaṟpaṭṭil caturamīṉāka uttiyōkañ ceytu aṅkilēyarāl naṉkoṭai palavum peṟṟu, tēka viyōkamāṉār. ivaruṭaiya kumāraṉ kiṟistavarākap paṭṭaṇattilirukkiṟār.

Pope) māttiram naṉṉūlilum vicēṣa nūl tamiḻilak kaṇattuk kuṇṭeṉat terintu, pala ilakkaṇaṅkaḷaiyum ārāyntu, tamatu pōṅkāy mūṉṟām ilakkaṇam (Pope's Third Grammar) oṉṟiyaṟṟi, atil yāpparuṅkalam, kuvalayāṉantam, ilakkaṇak kottu mutaliya pala nūlkaḷiṉiṉṟum ciṟitu ciṟiteṭuttuk kūṟiyirukkiṟār.



paṭṭaṇattil taṭṭār kula tilakarāyp piṟantu, anēka varuṣaṅkaḷāyt tiruccirāppaḷḷiyil tamiḻ kaṟpittu vanta muttuvīrak kavirāyar, mutal yāppilakkaṇam cūttirappāvil iyaṟṟi acciṭṭār. piṉ pañca vilakkaṇaṅkaḷaiyum cavistāramāyc cūttirappāvil, cuyamāy eḻuti acciyaṟṟum camaiyattut tēkaviyōkamāṉar. ivar tolkāppiyam, akattiyam, ilakkaṇa viḷakkam eṉṟa nūlkaḷiṉ cūttiraṅkaḷaik kōttu, uraiyil kāṇpavaṟṟaic cuyamāṉa cūttiraṅkaḷaik koṇṭu kāṇpittu,





arumpirayācaiyāy muṉṉōr moḻikaḷaip poṉ pōṟ pōṟṟi nalla nūloṉṟu muṭittār.



tamiḻ vaḻuvaṟakkaṟṟu, anēka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ pārvaiyiṭṭu, camaskirutamum terintukoṇṭu, ‘ilakkaṇattiraṭṭu’ eṉac ciṟanta vācaka ilakkaṇam iyaṟṟiyavar kaṉam. mārṭiṉ uvilpireṭ (Mr. Martyn Wilfred) eṉṟavar. ivar vekunāḷ upāttiyāyarākac carkkār paḷḷikkūṭaṅkaḷil nalla vēlai ceytu, paḷḷikkūṭa vicāraṇaicey (Deputy Inspector) vēlaiyilirukkiṟapōtē tēka viyōkamāṉar.



taṟkālattil tamiḻ apiviruttikkāka atikamāy uḻaittu varupavarkaḷ (Mr.W.Thamotharam Pillai, B.A., B. L., Mr. P. Ranganatha Mudaliar, M. A., Mr. J. M. Vellu Pillay) yāḻppāṇikaḷāṉa kaṉam. tāmōtaram piḷḷai, kaṉam. jē. vēluppiḷḷai, periyapaṭṭam peṟṟa kaṉam iraṅkanāta mutaliyār mutaliyavarkaḷē. ivaruḷ kaṉam. tamōtarampiḷḷai ēṭṭuppiratikaḷ maṅkimaṟaintu kiṭanta tamiḻ ilakkaṇa ilakkiyaṅkaḷil anēkattaik kaṇṇum tiruṣṭi kuṟaiyattakkatāka veku kaṣṭattuṭaṉ vācittut tiruttip pērt teḻuti, mikunta poruṭ celavum ceytu, acciyaṟṟit tamiḻ pāṣaiyiṉ ciṟappup piṟarukku viḷaṅka mikka karuttōṭu uḻaittu varukiṟār.





itu 1893 ām āṇṭu tāvitu yōcēpu upāttiyāyarāl eḻutappeṟṟa ‘tamiḻ ilakkaṇat teḷivu’ eṉṟa nūliliruntu eṭukkappeṟṟatu. akkālattiya urainaṭaikkum ituvē cāṉṟākiṉṟatu. itil akkālattil tamiḻ nāṭṭil vāḻntu moḻiyaiyum urainaṭaiyaiyum vaḷampaṭuttiya palarai avar kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. cilar āṅkilēyaraic cārntum cilar avarkaḷaic cārātum ciṟakka vāḻntuḷḷaṉar. āṅkilēyaraic cārātu vāḻtalē perumaiyuṭaiyateṉa akkālattil karutappaṭṭateṉpatai aṟikiṟōm. āṅkilēyaraic cārātu urimaiyōṭu tamiḻt toṇṭu ceytu vāḻnta periyavarkaḷaic ‘ciṅka’ṅkaḷeṉavē avar aḻaikkiṟār.

āṅkila aracu tamiḻ nāṭṭil pirapalyamāyirukkum pōtu, āṅkilēyarāl naṉku matikkappaṭṭuvanta tamiḻppaṇṭitarkaḷ irāmānujakkavirāyar, vicākap perumāḷaiyar, tāṇṭavarāya mutaliyār, a. capāpati mutaliyār, a. vīrācāmi ceṭṭiyār, vēlāyuta mutaliyār, caravaṇap perumāḷaiyar.



āṅkilēyarai aṭuttuṟavāṭātirunta tamiḻ ciṅkaṅkaḷ nallūr āṟumuka nāvalar, māyavaram mīṭṉāci cuntaram piḷḷai, vaṭalūr irāmaliṅkap paratēciyār, tiricirapuram muttuvīrak kavirāyar. ivarkaḷ koñca kālattukku muṉtāṉ[4] kālavi yōkamāṉarkaḷ. icciṅkaṅkaḷiṭam kaṟṟuc collikaḷāyiruntavarkaḷ ippōtu kalācālaikaḷil tamiḻ muṉṣikaḷāka virukkiṟārkaḷ.





ivvāṟu akkālap pulavarkaḷ pārāṭṭap peṟukiṟarkaḷ. ivarkaḷuḷ perumpālōr tamiḻ urainaṭaiyai vaḷampaṭuttiyavarkaḷē. eṉavē ivar tam urainaṭaic ciṟappukaḷai iṉṟum nāḷaiyum kāṇalām. āṅkilēyarukkāka amaikkappaṭṭa caṅkamē ceṉṉaik kalvic caṅkamākum.

ūr varalāṟukaḷ

mekkaṉci turai avarkaḷ palaviṭaṅkaḷukku nēril ceṉṟu kaṇṭavaippaṟṟi mēlēyaṟintōm. avaṟṟuḷ perumpālāṉavai avvap pakuti paṟṟiya kataikaḷum varalāṟum mutaliyaṉavumēyām. cilavaṟṟai makkaḷē eḻutik koṭuttārkaḷ. cilavaṟṟai nēril colla atikārikaḷ kuṟittuk koṇṭārkaḷ. oṉṟu kāṇpōm.

kēvai māvaṭṭattu antiyūr tālukkāvaic cērnta varatanallūr kirāma makkaḷ avvūriṉ paḻaiyavaralāṟṟiṉai “mekkaṉsi' turaikku nēril coṉṉatu oṉṟu. itu avar tokuppil citaiyātu uḷḷatu. toṭakkamum muṭivum maṭṭum īṇṭuk kuṟikkiṉṟēṉ.

⁰⁰⁰8⁰⁰7௵ (1807) ēpral௴ ⁰92 mējar karṉal mekkaṉsi cākipu avarkaḷ carkkārukkuk kōyamuttūr jillā antiyūr tālukkāviṟ cērnta akrakāram opaṉi 'pāvāṇikūṭal’ miṭṭar payicimavucē ‘varatanallūr’ akkirakāram makā jaṉaṅkaḷ eḻutikkoṭutta ‘kaipīyattu’ eṉṉaveṉṟāl:—

pūruvattil cālivākaṉa cakāptam ⁰⁰⁰ ⁰⁰ (1100). varuṣattukkumēl ūrāṭcikkōṭṭai kirāmattukkuc cērntatu ‘allappac ceṭṭi’ pāḷaiyam eṉṟu oru pēṭṭaiyiruntatu. ௸ pēṭṭaip pāḷaiyakkāra rājikattil kāliyāyp pōyiṟṟu.

appuṟam cakāptam ⁰⁰⁰3⁰⁰ kāḷayutti varuṣm tēvarāya makarāya mēṟpaṭi allappac ceṭṭi pāḷaiyattil allāḷappaṭṭar. caṅkama paṭṭar vakaiyarā pirāmaṇarkaḷukku akkirakāramum kaṭṭu vittuk koṭuttu ‘cōmakkirahaṉa’ puṇṇiya kālattil mēṟpaṭi pirāmaṇarkaḷukku muppattu nālu paṅkum tāṉam paṇṇic civappiratiṣṭaiyum paṇṇuvittuk koṭuttār.atu mutal vekutāṉiya varuṣam varaiyil nirupātika caruva māṉiyamāy naṭantu vantatu.

ivvāṟu toṭaṅkiya varalāṟu, piṉ palvēṟu viḷakkaṅkaḷ kāṭṭi aṭiyiṟ kaṇṭapaṭi muṭikiṉṟatu.

niṉṟa carkkār iraṇṭu paṅkukkuc carkkār māṉiyam pōṭṭuk koṇṭārkaḷ. atumutal etatkakṣaṇam varaiyil ௸ pūmi aṉupavittu varukiṟōm. itu naṭanta kaipīyattu. ik kaipītu eḻutiṉatu ௸yūr jaṉam pēr. kuppaṉ eḻuttu. irāmaiyaṉ kaiyeḻuttu. iraṅkaiyiṉ rucuvu, tirumalairāyaṉ rucuvu. nārāyaṇayyaṉ rucuvu (Mec. collections, D. 2877)

ivvāṟu ivvaralāṟṟuk kuṟippu muṭikiṉṟatu. itil kāṇum āṇṭuk kaṇakkuc cariyāka illai. itaik kaipiyattu allatu kaipītu eṉṟu kuṟittuḷḷaṉar. iccoṟkaḷukku vivarak kuṟippu eṉṟum statement, report, detailed account, particulars, eṉṟum poruḷ uḷḷaṉa. kaipīyattu nāmā eṉṟāl pirāmaṇap pāttiram allatu ‘Affidavit’ eṉṟu poruḷ.[5] 1839 il veḷiyāṉa nūlil 'news, particulars' eṉṟa poruḷkaḷ uḷḷaṉa[6]. eṉavē iccol iṉṟu nāṭṭil atikamāka vaḻakkattil illai eṉṟālum ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil kirāma makkaḷiṭattil mikac cātāraṇamāka vaḻaṅkiya oṉṟu eṉat terikiṉṟatu. itil eḻutiṉavar, cāṭci tantavar ākiyōr peyarkaḷum iṟutiyil iṭampeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ‘rucuvu’ eṉpatu cāṭciyākum. ‘miṭṭāpayici mavucē’ eṉpatu akkirāmam tāṉamākak koṭutta vakaiyai viḷakkum toṭarpōlum, ittakaiya ‘kaipītu’kaḷ ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil tamiḻ nāṭṭil atikamāka vaḻaṅki vantamaiyiṉ ic cācaṉa muṟaiyāl tamiḻ urainaṭai ōraḷavu vaḷarcci peṟṟuḷḷatai maṟukka muṭiyumō?

ivvāṟāṉa kaipītukaḷ eṇṇaṟṟavai eṉak kaṇṭōm. mēlē nām kaṇṭatu nēril colliyatu; ūr varalāṟu paṟṟiyatu. iṉi inta aṭippaṭaiyil mekkaṉci turai avarkaḷ ‘rayiṭṭar pāpu rāyar’ avaruṭaṉ ceṉṟu avvan nilattu makkaḷaik kēḷvi kēṭṭup patil peṟum vaḻakkamum uṇṭu eṉak kāṇkiṟōm. turai avarkaḷ eḻuttaruṭaṉ (writer) ceṉṟu makkaḷuṭaṉ kalantu paḻaki, avarkaḷai kēḷvikaḷ kēṭṭup peṟṟa patilkaḷ pala. avaṟṟuḷ cīraṅka viṉāviṭaiyaik kāṇpōm.

viviaiṭai-piratiyuttaram

1. vatu kēḷvi: tarmavarmā eṉappaṭṭavar yār? avartāṉā tarmarācar? avarālētāṉā ātiyil srīraṅkam kōyil ēṟpaṭṭatu? ‘varmā’ eṉpataṟku arttam eṉṉa?

uttaram:—tarmavarmā veṉkiṟavar cōḷa tēcattu rājā. avar tarmarājā aṉṟu. intak kaliyukattukku muṉ tuvāparārttattil tarma rājāviṉuṭaiya kālam. tarma varmāviṉuṭaiya kālam 3⁰6 caturyukattukku muṉ tirētāyukattu rājā, vipīṣaṇarukkuk koṭutta srīraṅka tivya vimāṉattukkut tarmavarmā captapkāram, maṇṭapam, kōpurātikaḷ kaṭṭiṉavar. ‘varmā’ eṉṟa captattukkuk kavacam eṉṟu arttam. ākac(cē) ceyta tarmattaik kavacamāka uṭaiyavar, (varma captam kṣattiriyaraik kuṟikkum oru kuṟippup pērām. atu carmā eṉpatu pirāmaṇaraik kuṟippatu pōlā meṉka.) tarma varmāviṉuṭaiya vācastāṉam uṟaiyūr. ataṟku nikaḷāpuri eṉṟum peyar. avar turaittaṉam mutaltāṉ antap paṭṭaṇattukkup piracitti. (Mec. Collections D. 3186) ivvāṟu tiruvaraṅka antaṇarkaḷ ‘mekkaṉsi’kku muṉ viḷakkum poḻutu cōḻar kālattai 36 yukattukku muṉpu (cumār 60 laṭcamx36) īrttuc cellukiṉṟaṉar. itupōṉṟa viḷakkaṅkaḷ pala avar kuṟippil uḷḷaṉa. cila cātikaḷ paṟṟiya kaipītukaḷum uḷḷaṉa. oṉṟu kuṟumpar paṟṟiyatu. avar ūrkaḷ aṉaittum iṉṟum ceṉṉaikkup pakkattilēyē uḷḷaṉa.

kuṟumparkaḷuṭaiya ﻿24﻿ kōṭṭaikaḷ uṇṭāyiruntaṉa. avaikaḷil upa kōṭṭaikaḷ atika vicēṣa kīrttikaḷaip peṟṟatu. inta irupattiṉālukkum puḻal kōṭṭai rājatāṉi eṉappaṭṭirukkutu. intak kuṟumparkaḷ pañca pāṇṭavarkaḷiṉ yuttattil aḷaṉākāḷar ākkappaṭṭu paṭukaḷattil tīkamāy toṇṭupaṭṭataṉāl yuttattukku atika paṇpaṭṭavarkaḷāṉārkaḷ āṉālivāḷanta yuttattil muṟiyaṭikkappaṭṭu eṅkum paravi citaṟaṭikkappaṭṭārkaḷ.

ivarkaḷ kollappaṭṭaviṭam neṭumpūril. anta iṭam iṉṟum kuṟumparaik koṉṟaviṭam eṉṟu collappaṭutu. kuṟumparukkuṇṭāṉa kōṭṭaikaḷiṉ vivaram poḻil–cālap pākkam–neṭumpūr – aṇaikkaṭṭu – paṭṭipulam – āmūr – puliyūr – maṟayicīkuḷam – kaḷattūr – cempūr–tamuṇuār–yīkārṭu.

(Mec. Collection D. 867)

varalāṟṟuk kataikaḷ

ivar tokuppil māṇikka vācakar paṟṟiya oru varalāṟṟuk kuṟippum kāṇappeṟukiṉṟatu. ataṉ

mukavuraiyaiyum mutalāvatu pattiyaiyum maṭṭum īṇṭuk kāṇalām. mukavurai: kaṉam poruntiya mēcar kālaṉ mekaṉsi turaiyavarkaḷ vētanāyaka vētākama cirōmaṇiyārukkuk kuṭutta tiṭṭam allatu memōraṇṭattiṉpaṭi māṇikkavācakar carittiram ēteṉpatait tiṭṭamāy vicāritteḻuta vēṇṭiyatāmē. itait toṭṭu aṟiya vēṇṭiyatu.

(ka. vatu) māṇikkavācakarteṭcaṇa rācciyattil maturaippaṭṭaṇattil pirāmaṇa vaṅki cattilē piṟantu teṉṉavaṉ piramarāyaṉ eṉkiṟa pāṇṭiyaṉukku mantiriyāyiruntu avaruṭaiya tiraviyattaik koṇṭu āviṭaiyār kōvileṉṟu collappaṭukiṟa tirupperuntuṟaiyilirukkappaṭṭa tiruppaṇikaḷaik kaṭṭiṉareṉṟum kaṭaiciyilē citamparattukku vantu avviṭattilē ﻿yīḻattu﻿ rācāviṉiṭattiliruntu vanta puttar camayattārai vātu paṇṇi allatu tarkkam paṇṇiyaḻittup pōṭṭāreṉṟuñ caiva matattārellām vāy vicēṣamākac collu kiṟārkaḷ.

ālaivar enta varuṣam enta āṇṭilē yiruntāreṉṟu vāy vicēṣamākac collukiṟa varkaḷaiyuṅ kāṇōm, purāṇattilum kāṇōm.

ivvāṟu māṇikkavācakar varalāṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. itil moḻit tavaṟu maṭṭumallātu iṭam, varalāṟu, peyar mutaliyaṉavum piḻaipaṭṭiruppataiyum kāṇkiṉṟōm. eṉiṉum ivaipaṟṟi yellām viḷaṅka aṟiyātavarākiya āṅkilēyarākiya mekkaṉci avarkaḷ avaravar eḻutiyataiyum kūṟiyataiyum appaṭiyē vakuttut tokuttu vaittār. ivvāṟu ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil cevi vaḻiyākavum eḻuttu vaḻiyākavum nāṭṭil vāḻntu vaḷarntu vanta urainaṭaiyai nammāl kāṇa muṭikiṉṟatu. inta urainaṭaiyil pala piḻaikaḷ uḷḷaṉavēṉum ituvum tamiḻ urainaṭai varalāṟṟil iṭam peṟṟup piṉ naṉku vaḷara irukkum urainaṭaikku vaḻikāṭṭiyāka amaiyavillaiyā!

makkaḷ piṇakku

iṉic cila makkaḷiṉ varalāṟu paṟṟiya kuṟippukkaḷaiyum kirāmaṅkaḷ tōṟum makkaḷ cātiveṟikoṇṭu māṟupaṭṭa varalāṟukaḷaiyum ikkuṟippukkaḷil kāṇkiṉṟāēm. ittuṟaiyil pala ivar tokuppil uḷḷaṉa. oṉṟai maṭṭum kāṇalām. iṉṟum caturaṅkappaṭṭiṉattai aṭutta nilappakutikkuk kuṟumparai nāṭu eṉṟum kuṟumpar nāṭu eṉṟum peyar iruppatai aṟivōm. āyiṉum kuṟumpar eṉṟa marapiṉar vāḻvaik kāṇal aritu. itaṟkāṉa kāraṇattai iṉṟum nammāl aṟiya muṭiyavillai. piṉvarum varalāṟṟuk kuṟippu- ataṉ urainaṭaiyil palvēṟu tavaṟukaḷ peṟṟiruntapōtilum-kuṟumpar vāḻvu vīḻnta varalāṟṟai appaṭiyē paṭam piṭittuk kāṭṭuvatu pōṉṟu amaintuḷḷatu.

kuṟumpar eṉṟa iṭavamisattāruṭaiya

﻿carittiram:





ṟāyar camastāṉattilē kuṟumpar eṉṟa yiṭac cātikaḷ vekuiṭaṅkaḷilē ṟāṭciyapārampaṇṇiṉārkaḷ. avāḷ neṭumaram, aṇaikkaṭṭu, cāla pākkam, nerumpūr, eṉṟa yiṭaṅkaḷilēyum yiṉṉam matta iṭaṅkaḷilēyum kōṭṭaikaḷ kaṭṭikkoṇṭu pirapaliyamāy vāḻntārkaḷ=aṉṉāḷkaḷilē kuṟumpar kuṭimakkaḷ mutalikkuṭikaḷaiyum veḷḷāḷakuṭikaḷaiyum taṅkaḷai vaṇaṅkumpaṭiyāṉa piṟaiyattiṉam paṇṇiṉārkaḷ āṉal mutalikaḷum veḷḷāḷarum atukkuc cammati koṭukkavillai appō kuṟumparkaḷ avāḷukku vekuyiṭukkaṅkaḷ paṇṇiṉārkaḷ





āṉālavāḷataṟkuc cammatikuṭukkavillai-atiṉ pērillavarkaḷ teruvilum muccantikaḷilum vettilai tōṭṭaṅkaḷilum yiṉṉam veku yiṭaṅkaḷilum tiṭṭi vācal pōlē kaṭṭi atukketiṟik kuṟumpar uḷukkārntu koṇṭu caṟuvattiṟāḷum atil talai kuṉintu nuḻaiyumpaṭiyākavum atiṉālē taṅkaḷukku vaṇakkam naṭakkavum ettiṉam paṇṇiṉarkaḷ-appō mutalimārum veḷḷāḷamārum talaikuṉintu nuḻaikiṟatukku vatiḷāka mutal kālai viṭṭu nuḻaintu yitaṉalē yavāḷai avacaṅkai paṇṇiṉarkaḷ-eṉṟa pōtaikkum avāḷatikārastarkaḷāṉapaṭiyiṉalē palavitattilum veḷḷāḷaraiyum mutalikaḷaiyum tuṉpappaṭuttiṉarkaḷ. appō tuṉpam atikarittapaṭiyiṉalē yivāḷellārum yōciṉai paṇṇi oṉṟilēyum muṭiyāmal kaṭaiciyilē yoru ampaṭṭaṉiṭattilē pōya avaṉukku māṉṉiya cāṉṉiyam paṇṇi vikkiruēm kuṟumparai yatampaṇṇa yōciṉai kēṭṭārkaḷ appō avaṉ camāḷippuc colli tāṉatukku upāyam paṇṇukiṟēṉ eṉṟu tiṭārikkañ colli yivāḷai vīṭṭukku aṉuppi viṭṭāṉ atiṉ piṟaku avaṉ taṉṉiṉattārākiya ammaṭṭar vīṭukaḷukkellām pōy avāḷellārum taṉakku utavi ceyya vēṇumeṉṟu kēṭṭuk koṇṭāṉ antappaṭiyē avāḷellārum utavi ceykiṟōmeṉṟu vākkuk kuṭuttārkaḷ kuṟumpa cātiyārukkuḷḷē oruvaṉ ceṉṟupōṉal iḻavukku varukiṟa vāḷak kūṭṭattāraṉaivōrum talaiyēkac cavaram paṇṇukiṟatu vaḻakkamāyiruntatu—



appaṭi orunāḷ ﻿taṟuvāyilē kuṟumparkaḷukkuḷḷē mūppāyirukkiṟa oruvaṉ viḻuntu pōṉaṉ appō kuṟumparkaḷ ellōrum





ēkōpittuk kūṭiyiruntu avaṉiḻavu caṭaṅku naṭappikkiṟatilē ellārum māmūlap paṭikku cavaram paṇṇikkoḷḷa oru mikka uḷukkārntārkaḷ appō yinta ammaṭṭaṉaṉavaṉ tāṉ pantu kaṭṭu paṇṇiyirunta ammaṭṭarkaḷ ellōrukkum tākkitai paṇṇi avāḷavāḷ ciṟaikkiṟa maṉuṣarai avāḷavāḷ koṉṟu pōṭak kaṟpittiruntāṉ antappaṭiyē yavāḷavāḷ ciṟaitta maṉuṣaṉai avaṉavaṉ talaiyai ciraittu kaḻuttaṇṭaiyil varukiṟapōtu kuravaḷaikaḷai aṟuttuk koṉṟu pōṭṭārkaḷ appōyiṅkē māṇṭupōṉa kuṟumpaṟuṭaiya srīkaḷellārum pelatta akkiṉi vaḷattu atil kutittu piruṇacētam paṇṇikkoṇ ṭārkaḷ-appō srīkaḷ nerumpūrilē muppōkam viḷaintālum uppukku neṟ kiṭaiyāl pōkaveṉṟu cāpamiṭṭārkaḷām-kuṟumparālē kaṭṭappaṭṭa kōṭṭaikaḷiṉṉam mēlākac collappaṭṭa yiṭaṅkaḷilē muḻutum yiṭintu taviḻntu maṇmēṭāyk kiṭakkutu atai pōyp pārttavarkaḷirukkiṟārkaḷ avāḷāl kaṭṭappaṭṭa kēṇikaḷ antanta yiṭaṅkaḷilē pāḻāyp pōyk kiṭakkutu atu yinta caturaṅkap paṭṭaṇattilēyum yirukkutu antak kalkaḷ pūṟuvaṅkaṇṭa veku nāḷaiyiṉ kallukaḷāyirukkutu—





(Mec, Collection, D. 3838)



pañca tantiram

inta aḷavōṭu ittakaiya kuṟippukkaḷai niṟutti vēṟu vakaiyil urainaṭai vaḷarnta varalāṟṟaik kāṇpōm. pañcatantirak kataiyāṉatu tāṇṭavarāya mutaliyār eṉpavarāl āṅkilēyar poruṭṭuc ceyyap peṟṟa oru nūl eṉpataik kaṇṭōm. innūlai 1825–il avar eḻuti muṭittār. eṉiṉum 1850-il avar maṟaintār eṉṟum kāṇkiṟōm. avarē kītā mañcari eṉṟa maṟṟāeru vacaṉa nūlum ilakkaṇa viṉā viṭai eṉṟāeru nūlum eḻutiṉār eṉpar. antap pañcatantiram eṉṟa katai āṅkilēyarukkākap paṭippaṭiyāka ilakkaṇap puṇarccikaḷ porunta amaiyap peṟṟuḷḷamaiyum mēlē kaṇṭōm. mutalil varum kataikaḷ eḷiya iyalpup puṇarvuṭaiya vākkiyaṅkaḷāka amaiya, piṉ varavarap palvēṟu ilakkaṇap puṇarvukaḷaiyuṭaiya vākkiyaṅkaḷ iṭam peṟṟaṉa. intak kataiyē ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭilum in nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattilum palvēṟu pāṭanūlkaḷil tavaṟātu iṭam peṟṟamaiyai aṟikiṟōm. ataṉ naṭai muṉ kaṇṭamai pōlaṉṟic cemmai nalam cārntirukkiṉṟatu. ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ urainaṭaikku atil oru ciṟu pakutiyaiyēṉum kāṇal tēvaiyākiṉṟatu. itō anta naṭaikkup puṇarcci ilakkaṇam nirampiya ōr eṭuttuk kāṭṭu:—

aintāvatu. acampirēṣiya kāriyattuvam–katai 1.

uccayiṉi purattil vatinta tēvanāma vētiyaṉoruvaṉ maṉaiviyōṭu illaṟam naṭāttum nāḷiṟ kuḻaviyillāk kuṟaiyāl iruvarum oru kīrippiḷḷaiyai maintaṉaippōṟ karuti vaḷarppuḻi oru makavuṇṭāyiṟṟu. maṉaivi ammatalaiyai ōr nāḷ tūṅkuvittuk kaṇavaṉaip pārttu, “yāṉ utakattiṉ poruṭṭuc cellā niṉṟēṉ. nīr naṅ kāṉ muḷaiyaip pārttirum, oru vēḷai kīri kaṭikkum” eṉṟiṅṅaṉam vakuttut taṇṇīrkkuc cella, piṉṉar akkuḻantai toṭṭiliṟ kaṇ vaḷarntatu avvantaṇaṉum oru taṭākak karuma vayattaṉayp puṟañ cella, attaruṇattil oru kiruṣṇa caruppam pilattiṉiṉṟum puṟampōntu toṭṭiliṉ mēṟ cellā niṟkak kaṇṇuṟṟa nakulam atai akkaṇamē viraintu paṟṟi utaṟik koṉṟu pala





kaṇṭalāṅkaṇṭu kurutiyāṟ pūcuṟṟa mukattuṭaṉē taṉatu āṟṟalaik kāṭṭa virumpi viraintu tuvāra mukamāka varukaiyil, vanta pārppiṉi cennīrāl niṟainta ataṉ vataṉattaip pārtta vaḷavil atu taṉ piḷḷaiyaik kaṭittu maṭittateṉṟu uḷḷit tuḷḷit tuṇukkuṟṟu nīrkkuṭañ citaivuṟumpaṭi ataṉ talaimēṟ pōṭṭuk koṉṟu uṭaṉē uṭpukā niṟkaiyil, makaṉ uṟaṅkutalaiyum arukē karumpāmpiṉ tuṇikkaik kuvaiyaiyuṅ kaṇṭu antō! keṭṭēṉ, yāṉ eṉ aruṅkuḻaviyai naṉkārāytaliṉṟik kolai ceytēṉeṉac cōṉamuṟṟup pulampā niṟpuḻi avaḷ talaivaṉ āṇṭu eyti, ‘kuḻantai itō irukkaiyil nī veṟumaiyeṉa makaviḻantē ṉeṉṟēṉ kataṟik kaluḻkiṉṟaṉa? eṉṉaṭi pētāy!” eṉa maṉaivi, “nīr yātō pēravāvāl eṉ moḻi taḷḷip pōṉamaiyāl ataṉ payaṉ intō vāyttatu, itu mikka avāviṉaṉ mūṭi mēṟ cakkiram cuḻaṉṟatai yottatu” eṉa, avaṉ ‘aḵtevvaṇṇam? uraitti’ eṉa uraippāṉ.





–pak. 131, 132.

kataikaḷ

ippaṭiyē vēṟu cila kataikaḷum vaḻakkattil uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟil oṉṟu mariyātairāmaṉ katai. 1847–il caitāppēṭṭai tēcappa ceṭṭiyār kumārar vīrapattira ceṭṭiyārāl jīvarakṣāmirta accakattil patippikkap peṟṟa katā cintāmaṇi eṉṟa nūlil oru pakuti itu. in nūl pala ciṟu kataikaḷait taṉṉakattē koṇṭatu. itil naṭai avvaḷavu ciṟantatāka illai yeṉiṉum ittakaiya kataikaḷaip potu makkaḷ virumpip paṭittārkaḷ eṉakkāṇa muṭikiṉṟatu. eṉavē ataiyum iṅkē tarukiṉṟēṉ.

mariyātai rāmaṉ katai:

⁰8 vatu. oruvaṉ kātuk kaṭukkaṉai maṟṟāevaṉ tiruṭiyatu.



oru cattirattal iraṇṭupēr vaḻippōkkar oruvaṉukkoruvaṉ etirmukamākat talaivaittup paṭuttuk koṇṭārkaḷ nittirai vēḷaiyilē valatu kātu kīḻāp paṭuttiruntavaṉuṭaiya iṭatu kātu kaṭukkaṉai iṭatu kātu kīḻākap paṭut tiruntavaṉ kaḻaṟṟit taṉ valatu kātilēpōṭṭuk koṇṭāṉ. viḻittukkoṇṭa piṟpāṭu kaṭutkaṉai yiḻantavaṉ eṉ kaṭukkaṉai ēṉ kaḻaṟṟik koṇṭā yeṉṟu kēṭka avaṉum eṉ kātuk kaṭukkaṉai nītāṉ kaḻaṟṟik koṇṭāyeṉṟāṉ, ippaṭik kalakappaṭṭuk kaṭukkaṉai yiḻantavaṉ mariyātai rāmaṉiṭattilē collik koṇṭāṉ. niyāyātipatiyākiya avaṉ tiruṭaṉai aḻaittuk kēṭṭaviṭattil avaṉum appaṭiyē coṉṉapaṭiyiṉalē nīṅkaḷ iruvarum eppaṭip paṭuttuk koṇṭīrkaḷō appaṭi pakuttuk koṇṭu kāṇpiyuṅkaḷeṉṟāṉ. appaṭiyē kāṇpittapōtu tiruṭaṉaip pārttu uṉ iṭatukātu kīḻē irunta paṭiyāl atiliruntu eppaṭi kaḻaṟṟalām. avaṉ iṭatu mēlē iruntapaṭiyāleḷitākak kaḻaṟṟik koṇṭāy. nītāṉ tiruṭaṉeṉṟu colli uṭamaikkāraṉukkuk kaṭukkaṉai vāṅkik koṭuttut tiruṭaṉē irupattunālu aṭi aṭippittu āṟu mātam kāvalirukkac ceytāṉ. attiruṭaṉ poy coṉṉalum poruntac collavēṉum eṉṟapaṭi kāttuk koḷḷavum teriyāmaṟ pōyiṟṟēyeṉṟu tuṉpamaṭaintāṉ.





﻿ ivaikaḷēyaṉṟi﻿ naṭēca cāstirikaḷ﻿ eḻutiya tirāviṭa nāṭṭup pūrvakālak kataikaḷ, mattiyakālak kataikaḷ mutaliyaṉavum paṭittu makiḻattakkaṉa. nalla urainaṭaiyil amaintuḷḷaṉ.

ivvāṟu ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil ettaṉaiyō vakaiyāṉa kataikaḷ eḻutappeṟṟum acciṭappeṟṟum nāṭṭil ulavivantaṉa. eḻutappeṟātu cevi vaḻiyākac ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭuvarai vanta palvēṟu kataikaḷum accuc cātaṉam uṇṭāṉa karāṇattālē nāṭṭil nūlkaḷāka uruppeṟṟu ulaviṉa. avai aṉaittaiyum varicaip paṭuttik kūṟuvateṉpatu iyalāta oṉṟu. mariyātairāmaṉ katai (1812). vikkiramātittaṉ katai, muppattiraṇṭu patumai katai (1804), catamuka rāvaṇaṉ katai (1818), tāṭi veṇṇeykkāraṉ katai (1843) viṟalimāṟaṉ katai (1828), tamiḻaṟiyum maṭantai katai (1812) mutaliyavai cila. ivaṟṟuḷ cila ēṭṭup paṭikaḷākavum iruntu inta nūṟṟāṇṭil acciṭappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivaiyuṅ ivai pōṉṟa eṇṇaṟṟa piṟa kataikaḷum nāvalkaḷum, vaḷarntu varukiṉṟa tamiḻ urainaṭai varalāṟṟil iṭam peṟattakkavaiyē eṉpatu maṭṭum uṟuti.

nāval

pattoṉpatām nūṟṟaṇṭilē, ittakaiya kataikaḷ maṭṭuṉṟi iṉṟu nāṭṭil vaḻaṅkum ‘nāval’ eṉṉum putuvakai nūlum iṭam peṟalāyiṟṟu. tamiḻil ‘piratāpa mutaliyār carittiram’ eṉṉum nāvalai eḻuti (1835 il) veḷiyiṭṭa māyūram vētanāyakam piḷḷai avarkaḷ ittakaiya nāvalkaḷ paṟṟiyum tamiḻil urainaṭai vaḷara vēṇṭiya tēvai paṟṟiyum tam mukavuraiyil kuṟittuḷḷār. atuvaraiyil tamiḻ urainaṭai vaḷarāmaikku uriya pala kāraṇaṅkaḷai viḷakki, ēṭṭil eḻuttāṇi koṇṭu eḻutum muṟaiyil nīṇṭa viḷakka urai eḻuta iyalāt taṉmaiyait toṭṭukkāṭṭi, tām urai naṭaiyil innāvalai eḻutiyataṟkuriya kāraṇantai avar viḷakkukiṟār.

My object in writing this work of fiction is to supply the want of prose works in Tamil, a want which is admitted and lamented by all.





(muṉṉurai)

urainaṭaiyiṉ tēvaiyai avar tamatu nūliṉ maṟṟoru pakutiyilum eḻutikkāṭṭiyuḷḷār. ‘vacaṉam’ eṉpataiyē avar āṇṭuḷḷār. ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil urainaṭaikku ivar eḻuttum ōr eṭuttukkāṭṭu.

vacaṉa kāviyaṅkaḷāl jaṉaṅkaḷ tirutta vēṇṭumē yallātu ceyyuṭkaḷaip paṭittut tiruntuvatu acāttiya mallavā? airōppiya pāṣaikaḷil vacaṉa kāviyaṅkaḷ illāmalirukkumāṉāl antat tēcaṅkaḷ nākarikamum naṟpāṅkum aṭaintirukkak kūṭumā? appaṭiyē nammuṭaiya cuya pāṣaikaḷil vacaṉa kāviyaṅkaḷ illāmalirukkiṟa varaiyil intat tēcam cariyāṉa cīrtiruttam aṭaiyāteṉpatu niccayam.





(42 ām atikāram.)

itil āciriyar kūṟiya aṭippaṭaiyilēyē pala urainaṭai nūlkaḷ ceṉṟa nūṟṟaṇṭil tōṉṟi vaḷarcciyuṟṟaṉa. ivvurainaṭaipaṟṟiyum nāval paṟṟiyum ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyilum inta nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattilum eḻunta cila viḷakkaṅkaḷ ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil irunta ataṉ nilaiyai naṉku viḷakkuvaṉavākum. ‘cāvittiri carittiram’ ‘patmāvati carittiram’ mutaliya nāvalkaḷ eḻutiya mātavaiyā avarkaḷ 1903 il ‘patmāvati carittiram’ iraṇṭām matippiṉ muṉṉuraiyil nāvalpaṟṟiyum ataṉ naṭaipaṟṟiyum kuṟikkiṉṟār. maṟṟellā uyartara kirantaṅkaḷaiyum pōlavē nāvaleṉṉum kirantamum paṭippavar





maṉataik kavarntu makiḻvūṭṭalai muṟkaruttākavum attuṭaṉ nallaṟivūṭṭalai uṭkarut tākavum koṇṭatu. kirantattiliṭaiyiṭaiyē kīḻttarappaṭṭapāttiraṅkaḷ pēcanērumpoḻutu ilakkaṇavaḻuc ceṟinta avartam vāymoḻikaḷai avvaṇṇamē eḻutuvatu vaḻakkamāyiṉum, kiranta karttā nēriṟ kūṟum varalāṟukaḷ vaḻuviṉṟiruttal vēṇṭum. kalvi tērcciyillātārum payaṉ peṟumpaṭi eḻutappaṭum kirantam ātaliṉ, evvaḷavu ilēcāṉa naṭaiyil eḻutappaṭiṉum nalamē āyiṉ eḷitil poruḷ teḷital karuti ilakkaṇa vaḻukkaḷōṭu eḻutappaṭiṉ paṭippavaruḷ ilakkaṇam payiṉṟār veṟuppaiyum, appayiṟciyillār—avarkaḷuḷ mukkiyamāy ivvitak kataikaḷaip perumpālum vācippōrākiya ilakkaṇa maṟiyāta peṇpālar—vaḻukkaḷai aṟiyamāṭṭārāy mayakkattaiyum aṭaivārātaliṉ naṉṉaṭaiyil eḻutuvatē takutiyākum.





(patmāvati carittiram. muṉṉurai)



ivvāṟu urainaṭai eḻutum pōtu makkaḷ kaiyāḷavēṇṭiya vakaiyiṉaiyum avaṟṟiṉ vaḻiyē ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil tōṉṟi urainaṭaiyai vaḷarttavar tiṟattaiyum kāṭṭiya, inta nūṟṟaṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil vāḻnta maṟṟeru pulavar karuttaiyum iṅkē kāṇalām. avar yāreṉṟu namakkut teriyātu. avar karuttum yāvarukkum ēṟkattakkatā eṉpatum ārāycciyillai. āyiṉum ceṉṟa nūṟṟaṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil urainaṭai vaḷarttavar paṟṟi inta nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil irunta intak karuttai maṭṭum kāṇalām.

vacaṉameḻutuvōr pirammasrī cūriyanārāyaṇa cāstiriyāriṉvaḻicceṉṟuvacaṉameḻutāmalirun tāltāṉ payaṉaṭaiyalām. cāstirikaḷ eḻutiya





vacaṉam mikavum kaṭiṉa naṭaiyuṭaiyatu. ammātiriyāṉa kaṭiṉa vacaṉam perumpālārkkup payaṉ paṭātu. “viḷaṅka vaittal” eṉpataik koṇṭu vacaṉam eḻuta vēṇṭum. kaviyāṉatu kaṭiṉamāyiruntālum kuṟṟamillai. vacaṉam kaṭiṉamāyiruntāl perum payaṉ aḷiyātu. ātaliṉ eḷiya naṭaiyil eḻutuvatutāṉ ucitam. srīmāṉ vētanāyakam piḷḷai, srīmāṉ celvak kēcavarāya mutaliyār ākiya vittuvāṉkaḷ eḻutiya vacaṉantāṉ namakku vaḻikāṭṭak kūṭiyatu. avarkaḷuṭaiya vacaṉa naṭai mikavum pārāṭṭaṟpālatu. ēṉeṉil, eḷiya naṭaiyil eḻutuvatu kaṭiṉa naṭaiyil eḻutuvataip pārkkilum varuttamāṉa kāriyam.



(rāmatās eṉṉum putiya kaṭṭuk katai—muṉṉurai, 1908)





ivvāṟu iṉṉum palarum ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ nāval paṟṟiyum urainaṭai paṟṟiyum pala karuttukkaḷaik kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. rupiṉcaṉ kurucō (1869), tacakumāra carittiram, pirēma kalāvatiyam mutaliya moḻipeyarppu nāvalkaḷum veḷivantuḷḷaṉa. ‘tīṉatayāḷu’ mutaliya nāvalkaḷum ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil vantaṉa. kirupai cattiyanātaṉ eṉṟa kiṟuttuva ammaiyārum iru nāvalkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷār. oṉṟu kiṟittava camayac cārpāṉatu; maṟṟatu intu camayac cārpāṉatu. ‘vinōta carittiram’ eṉṟu uyarnta ilakkiya naṭaiyil nārāyaṇacāmip piḷḷai eṉpār nāval eḻutiyuḷḷār eṉavum aṟikiṟōm. irājam aiyar avarkaḷ eḻutiya ‘kamalāmpāḷ carittiram’ nāṭaṟinta oṉṟu. 1875 mutal 25 āṇṭukaḷil cumār 50 nāvalkaḷ vantuḷḷaṉa. cila itaḻkaḷum palvēṟu toṭarkataikaḷaic ciṟukac ciṟuka veḷiyiṭṭu vantaṉa. ivvāṟu ciṟukataiyum nāvalum tāmum vaḷarntu tamiḻ urainaṭai vaḷaravum utaviṉa. makkaḷum ōraḷavu aṟivu viḷakkam peṟṟaṉar.

tiraṭṭu nūlkaḷ

ivvāṟu taṉittaṉiyāṉa kataikaḷiṉ amaippu maṭṭumaṉṟi, pala ciṟu kataikaḷait tokuttu veḷiyiṭṭa nūlkaḷum cila iruntaṉa. nalla pulavar eḻutiya kataikaḷ citaṟuṇṭupōkā vakaiyil pala aṟiñar avaṟṟait tokuttu vaittuḷḷaṉar. avaṟṟuḷ cila acciṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu aṟiñar em. es. pūraṇaliṅkam piḷḷai avarkaḷ tokuttatu. 1896il avar ‘vācakat tiraṭṭu’ eṉṟa peyaril pala katai kaṭṭuraikaḷait tokuttu veḷiyiṭṭār. avaṟṟuḷ cila pērilakkiyaṅkaḷiliruntu curukkamāka eḻutap peṟṟaṉa; cila moḻipeyarppukaḷum uḷḷaṉa. avai varumāṟu:



1. maṉōṉmaṇi katai jē. pi. vācutēva pantulu (cuntarampiḷḷaiyiṉ maṉōṉmaṇiyam)

2. cātumati caritai kē. kuppucāmi mutaliyār (King Lear)

3. maṉōkaravalli katai em. es. pūraṇaliṅkam piḷḷai (Teynnyson’s Princess)

4. taṟkāppu niyamam vi. kō. cūriyanārāyaṇa cāstiriyār (Herbert Spen-cer’s Principle of Ethics)

5. vaḷanāṭu vitvāṉ jekarāv mutaliyār (tēmpāvaṇi)

6. māṇikkam viṟṟap paṭalac curukkam ñāṉappirakāca vaḷḷaḷār (tiruvilaiyāṭal purāṇam)

7. aracaṉum pulavaṉum vi. kō. cūriyanārāyaṇa cāstiriyār (rūpāvati nāṭakam)

8. pirakalāta carittiram cīṉivācarākavācāriyār (viṣṇu purāṇam)

9. cāvittiri katai ṭi. es. kalacalōcaṉa ceṭṭiyār (pāratam)

10. srī rāmarum vāliyum aruṇācalak kavirāyar (irāmāyaṇam)

11. pāṇṭavar veḷippaṭutal caṇmukak kavirāyar (pāratam)

12. tantiram es. cuppiramaṇiyap piḷḷai (by Sri John Lubbork)

13. cīvakāruṇya viḷakkam citamparam irāmaliṅkam piḷḷai (aruṭpā)



ikkaṭṭuraikaḷai utaviya aṉaivarum ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil tamiḻ urainaṭai vaḷara utavi ceytavarkaḷē. ivaruḷ cilar pala perunūlkaḷaiyum yāttuḷḷamai aṟivōm. ivarkaḷuḷ oru cilaraip paṟṟi nāḷai teḷivākak kāṇalām. ivaṟṟil oriru cāṉṟu kāṭṭi iṉṟu amaiyalām. moḻipeyarppāl urainaṭai vaḷarttamaikkum itu oru cāṉṟākum. kataikaḷ vaḻaṅkiya vakaikkum avai cāṉṟu pakarum.

cātumati katai

ippūtalap pūvaikkut tilakam pōla viḷaṅkā niṉṟa virutai mānāṭu eṉṉum tivviya tēcattai maṉunīti tavaṟāmalum, ceṅkōl vaḻuvāmalum maṉṉuyirait taṉṉuyir pōl pātukāttu aracu purintu vanta vēntaṉoruvaṉ iruntāṉ. cūcai varmaṉ eṉṉum peyar pūṇṭa ivvaracaṉ taṉ maṉaiviyuṭaṉ illaṟam vaḻuvātu naṭatti, pūrva caṉmāntarattiṟ ceyta kaṉma vacattāṟ puttira pākkiyam peṟātu maṉavaruttamuṟuṅ kālattuk kaṇmaṇikaḷ pōṉṟa puttirikaḷ mūvar tōṉṟiṉar.

(mutal pakuti)

maṉōkaravalli katai

﻿teṉ tēcamāṇṭu vanta kuṇa vīraṉeṉṉum vēntaṉukku maṉēkaravalli eṉṟa arumaip putalvi utittaṉaḷ. avaḷ tālāṭṭup paruvattilēyē taṉ aṉṉaiyai iḻakkap peṟṟa nimittama, avaḷ pitā avaḷai nāḷoru mēṉiyāy vaḷarttut tāyiḻanta taṉi nilai maṉattiṟ ciṟitum pukāvaṇṇam avaḷ avāk koṇṭu kēṭkum aṉaittaiyum aṭṭiyiṉṟi avaṭkuc cēkarittuk koṭuttu avaḷ maṉōṣṭap paṭiyē naṭantu vantāṉ,

ittakaiya tokai nūlkaḷāl-ciṟappākak katai nūlkaḷāl-ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil tamiḻ urainaṭai naṉku vaḷarntatu eṉpatu uṟuti.

nāṭakam

ciṟukataikaḷ, nāvalkaḷ maṭṭumiṉṟi nāṭakaṅkaḷ pala ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil veḷivantaṉa. avai perumpālum urainaṭai nūlkaḷē. oru cila pāṭalkaḷ iṭaiyiṭaiyē viravap peṟṟaṉa. cila nāṭakaṅkaḷ muḻuvatum pāṭalāl āṉapōtilum toṭakka urai, aṟimuka uraikaḷ aṉaittum urainaṭaiyilēyē amaintuḷḷaṉa. attakaiya nāṭakaṅkaḷ palavāka nāṭṭil naṭikkappeṟṟaṉa. eṉṟālum oru cilavē acciṭappeṟṟu nūl vaṭivil vantuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu ‘tāciltār’ eṉṉum nāṭakam. itaṉ āciriyar māvaṭṭa naṭuvarāka (District Munsift) iruntavar. aracāṅka ūḻiyarkaḷil cilar naṭantu koṇṭa vakaiyai nēril kaṇṭu kaṇṇīr vaṭittavar eṉat terikiṟatu. itaṉ mutal patippu 1857lum iraṇṭām patippu 1868lum veḷi vantuḷḷaṉa. innūl avar makaṉārāl veḷiyiṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu. avarum ōyvupeṟṟiruntār eṉak kāṇkiṉṟāēm. nūliṉ mukappup pakkattil piṉvarum kuṟippu uḷḷatu.

tāciltār nāṭakam

caitāpuram kāci visvanāta mutaliyār iyaṟṟiyatu

iḵtu kōyamputtuār jillā uṭumalaip pēṭṭai muṉicīpāyiruntu ippōtu kevarṉmeṇṭu upakārac campaḷam peṟṟuk koṇṭirukkiṟa caitāpuram kācivisvanāta mutaliyār avarkaḷāliyaṟṟappaṭṭu avar puttiraṉākiya cōmacuntara mutaliyāratu 'sṭār āp intiyā pirasil' iraṇṭāvatu muṟai acciṟ patippikkappaṭṭatu.

⁰⁰⁰8⁰⁰⁰8 ௵ jaṉavari mātam.

103, Armenian Street,

Copy right.

inta mutaṟ pakka viḷakkam akkāla urainaṭaikkum cāṉṟāka amaikiṉṟatu. itaṉ muṉṉuraiyil āciriyar innāṭakam eḻuta nērnta varalāṟṟaik kūṟuvatum aṟiya vēṇṭuvatākum.

mukavurai: cilar oru erumaiyāṉatu taṇṇīr kuṭikkap pōṉa kuṭṭaiyaik kalakkic cērākkit taṉṉuṭalelāñ cēṟṟaip pūcikkoṇṭu naṭakkiṟa vaḻiyelāñ cēṟākki, vaḻiyiṟ pōkiṟavarkaḷ varukiṟavarkaḷ pēriluñ cēṟṟaip

pūcittaṉṉaik kaṭṭukiṟa koṭṭattil – kaṭṭuttaṟi – pul – itukaḷaiyellāñ cēṟākki – kaṭṭip pul pōṭa vanta taṉ ejamāṉaṉukkuñ cēṟu pūci vaippatu pōlak kiṭaitta uttiyōkattil paṇa ācaiyiṉāl lañcam vāṅka ārampittut taṅkaḷuṭaiya pērkaḷaiyum turaittaṉattārkaḷuṭaiya pērkaḷaiyum keṭuppatumallāmal anēka akkiramaṅkaḷukku uṭpaṭṭu anēka jaṉaṅkaḷ aniyāyamum tuṉpamum naṣṭamum aṭaiyumpaṭi ceytu varukaiyāl avaikaḷaip pala vivakāraṅkaḷilum, avaravar naṭattaikaḷilumaṟintum aṉupavamuṭaiya anēkarālum nontavarkaḷālum collak kēṭṭum uṇarntavaṉā yirukkiṟēṉ.

inta nāṭakattil pāṭalkaḷukkiṭaiyil uḷḷa urainaṭaip pakutikaḷ cilavaṟṟāl urainaṭai paṟṟiyum nāṭṭu nilai paṟṟiyum aṟikiṉṟāēm.

﻿(1) 6vatu pakkam, 5 vatu variyil cēra vēṇṭiya nōṭṭu,

kuṭikaḷuṭaiya cavukkiyattukkākak kuṭikiṇaṟukaḷai marāmattuc ceyyac carkkāril 5⁰–6⁰ rūpāy koṭutta pōtilum vīṭṭukkoru oṭṭaṉ veṭṭikku vantu vēlai ceytu pōkiṟatuṇṭu. ⁰⁰⁰8⁰⁰4⁰4 ākasṭu ௴ 82 riviṉyū pōrṭṭu carkkiyular uttaraviṉāl kūlikkāraraip palavantamākap piṭittu vēlaivāṅkivarum vāṭikkaiyai niṟuttak kaṭṭaḷai. ⁰⁰⁰8⁰⁰4⁰2 ௵ mē ௴ 92 uttaraviṉal taṇṭu caraparāvukku vaṇṭikkārarait tontaravupaṭuttak kūṭātu. vāṭakai cariyāyum tāmacamillāmalum paṭṭavāṭa ceyyak kaṭṭaḷai.

﻿(2) 8-vatu pakkam, ⁰4-vatu variyil cēravēṇṭiya nōṭṭu, ⁰⁰⁰8⁰⁰5⁰6 ௵ mē ௴ 42 riviṉiyu pōrṭṭu carkkiyular uttaraviṉāl



kalviyum naṉṉaṭattaiyumuḷḷōraip pārttut tācīl uttiyōkattukku niyamittu aluvalkaḷai nitāṉamā yōcittu naṭattumpaṭic ceyyak kaṭṭaḷai.

(3) ⁰–vatu pakkam, 27–vatu variyil cēra vēṇṭiya nōṭṭu. riviṉyu ṭipārṭṭumeṇṭil perumpālum tēcasta pirāmaṇāḷ irukkiṟārkaḷeṉpataik kāṭṭat kūṟiyatu. ⁰⁰⁰8⁰⁰5⁰5 ௵ mārcci ௴ 52 riviṉiyu pōrṭṭu uttaraviṉāl kalakṭṭar kaccēriyil iraṇṭu cirastātārarkaḷum tēcasta pirāmaṇāḷāka irukkak kūṭātu. pirāmaṇāḷaip pōlavē maṟṟa jāti jaṉaṅkaḷum tācīltārarkaḷāka niyamikka vēṇumeṉṟu kaṭṭaḷai.

﻿(4) ⁰2–vatu pakkam, 4–vatu variyil cēra vēṇṭiya nōṭṭu. ⁰⁰⁰8⁰⁰5⁰ ௵ navampar ௴ ⁰42 uttaraviṉāl muṉ eccarikkai koṭāmala tālukā kajāṉāvai appōtaik kappōtu kalaṭṭarkaḷ jaṭti pārttu varavēṇṭumeṉak kaṭṭaḷai.

(itil varum eṇkaḷ aṉaittum tamiḻ eṇkaḷākavē uḷḷaṉa.)

﻿(5) kuṟippu: tāciltār mikunta kōpattōṭu ēmakkāḷai utaikkac colliccoṉṉa māttirattil yamakiṅkirarkaḷpōla avariṭattilirukkum cēvakaril – raṅkaṉ – ēmakkāḷaik kaṉṉattilaṭikka, kavaṉappaṉ – aṭivayiṟṟil kālālutaikka, ēmakkāḷ–pēccaṟṟu mūccittut taraiyil viḻuntapōtu cēvakar collukiṟa vitaṅ kāṇka.

— aṭuttatu pāṭṭu (tāḷarākattuṭaṉ)



itupōṉṟa nāṭakaṅkaḷ ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil veḷi vantuḷḷaṉa. aṇmaiyil maṟainta pammal campanta mutaliyāratu nāṭakaṅkaḷum ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭilēyē nāṭṭil ulavat toṭaṅkiya nilaiyaiyum aṟikiṟām. avartam līlāvati culōcaṉā nāṭakam 1895il acciṭap peṟṟatākum. atil āciriyar tam muṉṉuraiyil,

﻿teṉṉāṭṭiṟkuriya tamiḻ ilakkiyamāṉatu iyal icai nāṭakameṉa namatu muṉṉērāl mūvakaiyākap pirikkappaṭṭirunta pōtilum taṟkālattil yātu kāraṇam paṟṟiyō nāṭakat tamiḻ atika vaḻakkattil illāmai kaṇṭu ciṟiyēṉ eṉatu ciṟṟaṟivaik koṇṭu ‘līlāvati-culōcaṉā allatu iraṇṭu cakōtarikaḷ’ eṉṉum ōr navīṉa nāṭakattai iyaṟṟiṉēṉ. taṟkālattiya nilaimaikkut takkatāyirukkum vaṇṇam āṅkilēya pāṣaiyiluḷḷa nāṭakaṅkaḷiṉ taṉmai vāyntatāyum nammavar ellōrum vācittaṟiyumpaṭi eḷiya naṭaiyāka vacaṉattilum innāṭakam eḻutappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.

eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟār. ivar naṭai mika eḷitāka ellōrum aṟintu koḷḷakkūṭiyatu. itupaṟṟi oṉṟum kāṭṭat tēvaiyillai.

tamiḻil pāṭanūl eḻutap paricu

pammal campanta mutaliyāriṉ tantaiyārākiya vijayaraṅka mutaliyārum tamiḻ urai vaḷarccikku utaviṉār eṉpataiyum iṅkē kuṟippiṭal poruttamāṉatākum. ‘amerikkā kaṇṭattaik kaṇṭupiṭitta varalāṟu’ paṟṟiya nūl oṉṟu vijayaraṅka mutaliyārāl 1852il eḻuti veḷiyiṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu. avartam muṉṉuraiyil tam āciriyar tiruttaṇikai vicākap perumāḷaiyar eṉak kuṟippiṭukiṟār. innūlum avarāl paricōtikkappaṭṭatu eṉak kuṟikkiṉṟār. avar tamatu mukavuraiyil,

﻿innūl irāparṭcaṉ eṉpavarāl iṅkilīṣil eḻutappaṭṭa amerikkak kaṇṭattiṉ caritattiliruntu eṭutteḻutappaṭṭatu. iḵtu ceṉṉap paṭṭaṇam yūṉivarciṭi eṉṉum cāstirak kalvic cālaiyil iṅkilīṣ mutaliya pāṣaiyaik kaṟkum māṇākkaril, iṅkilīṣ pāṣaiyiliruntu yātorupayōkamāṉa nūlait tamiḻ mutaliya pāṣaikaḷil naṉṟāka eḻutuvōrukkum aḷikkappaṭumpaṭi kā. paccaiyappa mutaliyār avarkaḷ tarumattai vicāraṇai ceyyuñ capaiyārāl mēṟpaṭi cāstirak kalviccālai akkirā caṉātipati attiyakṣar kaḷiṭattil vaikkappaṭṭirukkum poruḷ tokaiyil vilaipeṟṟa pustakaṅkaḷaip peṟumpaṭi inta varuṣārampattil eṉṉāl eḻutappaṭṭatu: (1852)

eṉṟu viḷakkantarukkiṉṟār. eṉavē akkālattilēyē pāṭanūlkaḷait tamiḻākkuvōrukkup paricaḷikkap paccaiyappar aṟanilaiyattār ippalkalaik kaḻakattāriṭam oru tokaiyait tantuḷḷārkaḷ eṉak kāṇkiṟōm. atu paṟṟi āyntu aṟital tēvaiyāṉa oṉṟu.

campanta mutaliyār nāṭakaṅkaḷuṭaṉ vaittu eṇṇat takka vakaiyil pala nāṭakaṅkaḷ ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil tōṉṟiṉa. avaṟṟuḷ piratāpa cantira vilācam eṉpatu camūka campantamāṉa oṉṟu. pa. va. irāmacāmi rājū, pi. ē., pārisṭar-aṭ-lā eṉpavar eḻutiya nāṭakam itu. itu eḻutappeṟṟa kālam tiṭṭamāka aṟiya muṭiyāviṭṭālum avar maṟaivu 17—8—1897 eṉa aṟivatāl ataṟku muṉpē itu eḻutappaṭṭatu eṉpatu tuṇipu. ittakaiya nāṭakaṅkaḷaṉṟi vaḷḷiyammai nāṭakam, iṭampāccāri vilācam, mataṉacuntara piratāpa cantāṉa vilācam mutaliya cumār 35 nāṭakaṅkaḷ nāṭṭil vaḻakkattil iruntaṉa. ivai aṉaittum ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil tamiḻ urainaṭai vaḷarccikku utaviṉa eṉpatu kaṇ kūṭu.



ittakaiya nāṭakaṅkaḷ, nāval, ciṟukataikaḷ tavirttuc ‘carittiraṅkaḷ’ eṉappaṭṭaṉavum cila accilum ēṭṭilum ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil ulavi vantuḷḷaṉa. tērūrnta cōḻaṉ carittiram, civāji carittiram, nācikētu carittiram (1880), maturaic caṅkattār carittiram, nālu mantiri carittiram (1839), ṭilli rājākkaḷ carittiram mutaliya varalāṟu kalanta kataikaḷ urainaṭaiyil iṭampeṟṟu nāṭṭil vāḻntu vantaṉa. avai iṉṟaiya carittira nāvalkaḷ pōṉṟaṉapōlum. ivvāṟu, potumakkaḷ eḷitil uṇarntu aṟintu koḷḷum vakaiyil pala ciṟukataikaḷum nāvalkaḷum nāṭakaṅkaḷum ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟi makkaḷ aṟivai vaḷarttatōṭu tamiḻ urai naṭai taḻaikkavum cātaṉamāka iruntaṉa. potumakkaḷiṉ kaṭitappōkku varavu vaḷarnta kāraṇattāl attuṟaiyiṉ vaḻiyum urainaṭai vaḷarntatu eṉpatai nāḷai kāṇalām. ittuṟaikaḷil kaṭanta pala nūṟṟaṇṭukaḷaikkāṭṭilum ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil tamiḻ urainaṭai atika vaḷarcci aṭaintatu eṉalām.

aracāṅka veḷiyīṭukaḷ

iṉi, aracāṅkattār tamiḻ urainaṭai vaḷartta vakaiyiṉaik kāṇpōm. aracāṅka viḷamparaṅkaḷ, uttaravukaḷ, pattiraṅkaḷ, caṭṭaṅkaḷ, kirāmak kaṇakkukaḷ, veḷiyīṭukaḷ mutaliya pala ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil–tamiḻil–urainaṭaiyil veḷivantuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ pala vaṭacoṟkaḷ maṭṭumaṉṟi arapiya, urutu, parciyac coṟkaḷum iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. mukalāya āṭciyai oṭṭi amainta kālamātalāl anta vāṭaiyiṉai aṅkē kāṇamuṭikiṉṟatu. attakaiya coṟkaḷuḷ cilavaṟṟaip piṉ tantuḷḷēṉ. aracāṅka veḷiyīṭukaḷai oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟākak kāṇalām. avai potuvākavum ciṟappāṉa oṉṟaip paṟṟiyaṉavumāka vuḷḷaṉa; viḷamparam, ‘ṭeṇṭar’, nalattuṟai, caṭṭam, mīṉpaṇṇai, kāvaltuṟai vaṇikattuṟai, cālaittuṟai, ūrāṭci, nāṭṭāṭci . nakarāṭci mutaliya pala tuṟaikaḷaip paṟṟiyaṉavāka uḷḷaṉa. potuvākac ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil ellāttuṟaikaḷilum aracāṅkattār āṅkilattil maṭṭumaṉṟit tamiḻilēyum ellā naṭavaṭikkaikaḷaiyum mēṟkoṇṭaṉar eṉpatu teḷivu.

(1) caṭṭanūl muṉṉurai: 1882 ām ௵ttiya 8vatu ākṭiṉāl tiruttupāṭāṉa intiya pīṉal kōṭāṉatu 1883m ௵ aiṉavari ௴ 12௳ mutal amulukkuk koṇṭu varappaṭṭatu.

(4)﻿kalvi paṟṟi

itiṉālē cakalamāṉa jaṉaṅkaḷukkum teriyappaṭuttukiṟa teṉṉaveṉṟāl varukiṟa jaṉavari mātam mutal tēti mutal haiskūlil paṭikkum vittiyārttikaḷiṉ campaḷam mātam oṉṟukku iraṇṭu rūpāyākak kuṟaikkappaṭum- mēlum orē kuṭumpattil oruvaṉukku atikamākap paṭikkum pakṣattil appaṭikku atikamāka varum vittiyārttikaḷiṭattil ௴ 1-kku oru rūpāy campaḷam vāṅkik koḷḷappaṭum.

varukiṟa varuṣam piṟanta mēl kūṭiyatu varaiyil ārampa cikṣaiyiṉ poruṭṭu haiyiskūlaic cārntatāy piraimērīskūl eṉṉum oru kalviccālai yēṟpaṭuttappaṭum. atu cilakāla pariyantam haiskū lippōtirukkum iṭattilēyē vaikkappaṭum.



mēṟkaṇṭa piraimēri skūl eṉṉum kalviccālaiyil kaṟkuñ ciṟuvaṉiṭattil mātam oṉṟukku oru rūpāy vītam campaḷam vāṅkik koḷḷappaṭum . pālarkaḷ paṭikkum puttakaṅkaḷai varukiṟa jaṉavari mātam mutal avāḷē contamāy vāṅkikkoḷḷa vēṇṭiyatu.

yūṉivarsiṭi pōrṭār vicāraṇaiyiṉ kīḻirukkiṟa kalvic cālaikaḷiṉ āṟu mācattiṟkorutaraṅ koṭukkum viṭumuṟai tinaṅkaḷukkaṭutta oru oru pakṣattiṟkuḷḷāka māttiramē vittiyārttikaḷai aṅkīkarikkumpaṭiyāy anta capaiyārukku eṇṇamuṇṭāyirukkiṟatu. ataik kuṟittiṉimēl teriyapaṭuttupvārkaḷ-āṉālippōtu terivikkiṟa teṉṉaveṉil varukiṟa āṟu mātattil haiyiskūlukkāvatu piraimēri skūlukkāvatu puttirarkaḷēyāvatu pantukkaḷaiyāvatu aṉuppa virumpukiṟavarkaḷellām haiskūl eṭmēsṭarukku varukiṟa mī ⁰82 yilāvatu ataṟku muṉṉāvatu teriyappaṭutta vēṇṭiyatu.

(10)﻿ aḷam kaṇakku:—(pala eḻuttukkaḷukkup puḷḷiyillai)

ceṉṉappaṭṭaṇam kalekṭarākiya ma-⁰⁰-⁰⁰ ē rōparṭcaṉ turaiyavarkaḷ camukattukku eṇṇūr aḷam kaṇakku muttappiḷḷai eḻutik koṭutta attāṭci.

eṉṉaveṉṟāl eṇṇūr aḷaṅkuṭiyākiya tōṇi ciṉṉaveṅkaṭṭarāmaṉ, iruṅkuṉṟam kiruṣṇappa nā. kaiyil matattuvāṅkik koṇṭatāy, taṉ pērilirukkiṟa aḷam-kate-um avaṉ pērālum carkkār kaṇakkil tākkal ceytu koṭukka vēṇumeṉṟu ācūril arji koṭukka anta arijip paṭikkuc ceytu koṭukkak kūṭā teṉṟu, muṉṉē akṣēpittiruntavarkaḷākiya, mēṟpaṭi ciṉṉa veṅkaṭṭa

rāmaṉuṭaiya takappaṉ tōṇimallā reṭṭiyum mēṟpaṭi veṅkaṭṭarāmaṉ peṇ cātiyākiya kāmākṣiyammāḷum, ippōtu cammatippaṭṭu, mēṟpaṭi kiruṣṇappa nā. pēraiyum carkkār kaṇakkil tākkal ceytukoḷḷalāmeṉṟu maṟupaṭiyum ujūril arj koṭuttuk kēṭṭuk koḷḷukiṟapaṭiyālum ௸ aḷattukkāka vēṟē āṣēpaṇai oṉṟumillāta paṭiyālum ௸ kiruṣṇappa nā. pēraiyum carkkār kaṇakkil tākkal ceytukoḷḷukiṟa tiṉālē yātoru vittiyācamu millai. intappaṭi nāṉ cammatittu attāṭci

aḷattuk kaṇakku

eṇṇūr

(17—12—1837) mutta pi.



itaṟkut tanta aracāṅka uttaraviṉaiyum uṭaṉ kāṇal naṉṟu:-

ijatācār muttucāmi piḷḷai aḷattu atikāri koṭṭār eṇṇūr.

tarivillā eṇṇūr aḷaṅkuṭi tōṇi ciṉṉa veṅkaṭṭarāmaṉ pērālirukkiṟa-ka te-um, iruṅkuṉṟam kiruṣṇappa nā. pērālum, kūṭṭāy carkkār kaṇakkil tākkal ceyvataṟku, yātoru ākṣēpaṇaiyumillai yeṉṟu nīr ௸ ௴ ⁰82 ummuṭaiya aāj rippōrṭu ceytirukkiṟa paṭikku ௸ ciṉṉa veṅkaṭṭarāmaṉ pērāl carkkār kaṇakkil tākkalāyirukkiṟa-kate-atu iruṅ kuṉṟam kiruṣṇappa nā. pērum ௸ tētiyil arj rijiṣṭar pustakattil takkal ceytirukkiṟōm ākaiyār koṭṭār majkūr aḷam ācāmi

vāri pustakattilum, kaṇakkuppiḷḷai kaṇakkilum, antappaṭikku tākkal ceyvittu, avarkaḷaik koṇṭu vārakkam cākupaṭi mutalāṉa kāriyamum jākkirataiyāy naṭappittuk koṇṭu varavum.

mukām,

kalekṭar



cila vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷ

akkālattil-ceṉṟa nūṟṟaṇṭil-vaḻaṅkiya cila coṟkaḷukkuriya poruḷkaḷai aṟitalum nalamākum. avaṟṟuḷ oru cilavaṟṟaik kīḻē tarukiṟēṉ. ikkālattilum perumpālum vaḻakkattil uḷḷa coṟkaḷai īṇṭu nāṉ kāṭṭavillai. ivai piṟamoḻikaḷiliruntu tamiḻil-ciṟappāka aracāṅkattuṟaiyil-vaḻaṅkiya coṟkaḷākum. ‘aṟamaṉai vārttaikaḷ’ eṉavē avai kuṟikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.

camutāya nūlkaḷ

ivaimaṭṭumaṉṟi makkaḷ camutāya vāḻvu paṟṟiya pala nūlkaḷ urainaṭaiyil vantuḷḷaṉa. 1898il ‘intiyaṉ kirimiṉal lāc curukkam’ eṉṟa nūl tamiḻil veḷiyāyiṟṟu. innūl kumpakōṇam eṉ. vaittiyanāta aiyarāl, pōlīcu āpīcarkaḷ, kirāma muṉicipukaḷ, pirai vēṭ vakkīlkaḷ mutaliya kirimiṉal viyavakāra ñāṉamaṭaiya virumpuvōr yāvarukkum mukkiya upayōkappaṭumpaṭi iyaṟṟappaṭṭatu’ ivvāṟu 1897il es. kumāracāmi mutaliyār eṉapavarāl 'jamīntāri eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟa stitiyaic cārnta caṅkiraka kirantam’ eṉṟa nūl jamīntār muṟai paṟṟi eḻutap peṟṟup pativu ceyyap peṟṟatu. ivai maṭṭumiṉṟi, 'vitavā vivākam’ pōṉṟa cīrtiruttaṅkaḷ paṟṟiya nūlkaḷum eḻuntaṉa. annūlkaḷukku aṭippaṭai akkālattu attakaiya tirumaṇaṅkaḷ naṭaipeṟṟamaiyē eṉpatu annūlkaḷaip payilvār naṉku uṇarvārkaḷ. nākarkōyilaic cērnta cēṣaiyaṅkār tam makaṉukku ittakaiya maṇam ceytār eṉṟum atai ātarittu 1874il 'vitavōtvāka vivēkam’ eṉṟa nūl eḻutappaṭṭateṉṟum aṟikiṟōm. annūlai maṟuttu aṭutta āṇṭē (1875il) raṅkāc raṅkāccāri eṉpār ‘vitavōtvāka kaṇṭaṉam’ eṉṟa nūlai eḻuti acceyalaik kaṇṭittirukkiṟar.

kaṇavaṉai iḻanta kaimpeṇṇaic cuṟṟi aḻuvataip paṟṟiya kuṟippu oṉṟu amarampēṭu muttucāmi mutaliyār eḻutiya camūka iyal paṟṟiya ‘apimāṉam eṉpatu eṉṉa?’ eṉṟa 1886il veḷiyāṉa nūlil uḷḷatu. itupōṉṟē vāḻviyalaip paṟṟiya pala kuṟippukaḷ innūlil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.

aḻukai:- peṇkaḷ mayiriḻavu, māriḻavu, kālaṭavu, kaṭṭiyaḻal eṉṉum ācāraṅkaḷai naṭattukaiyil, kaṭṭiyaḻum pōtu vācal peritāyirukkumāyiṉ, tiṭṭutiṭṭākap pattup patiṉaintu pēr vaṭṭamāka niṉṟu, kaikōttu acaintāṭi oruvita rākattōṭu pāṭik koṇṭu, koñca kālaṅ kaḻinta piṟaku, vēṟu naṭaic cantattōṭu pāṭikkoṇṭu, kālaṭavu pōṭṭu mārpilaṭittuk koṇṭu cuṟṟi varukaiyil putitāyk kaṟṟuk koḷḷukiṟavaḷāvatu vitaraṇai illātavaḷākavāvatu iruntu, aṭavu cariyāyp pōṭātavaḷaik kaiyaippiṭittiḻuttu veḷiyil viṭṭu viṭukiṟarkaḷ. iḻavukku aḻappōkaiyil talaimayirai cikkalillāmal uṭaittuk kōtikkoṇṭu pōy, etiretir mukamāka niṉṟu, etukai mōṉai tavaṟāmal ataṟkicainta vēṟu rākattuṭaṉē pāṭṭukkaḷ pāṭikkoṇṭu talaimayirai iraṇṭu kaikaḷālum viralkaḷaik koṇṭu cīvukiṟārkaḷ. kaṇavaṉaip paṟikoṭuttut tavikkiṉṟavaḷaik kīḻē taḷḷi maṟṟavarkaḷ avaḷaic cūḻntu ‘lapalapa'veṉṟu vāyilaṭittuk koḷḷukiṟarkaḷ. mārmēlirukkum tuṇiyai eṭuttu iṭuppil varaintu kaṭṭik koṇṭum mārpilaṭittuk koḷḷukiṟārkaḷ.

ivvāṟē peṇkaḷ malaṭāka uḷḷaṉarā, āṇkaḷ malaṭāka uḷḷaṉarā eṉpatai aṟiyac ‘ciṟṟiṉpac cirōṉmaṇi’ eṉṟa vacaṉa nūlil vaḻivakuttuḷḷaṉar. innūl ‘va. u. ci.’ avarkaḷāl avartam toṭakka kālattil eḻutappaṭṭatām. inta muṟai iṉṟuḷḷa pala ṭākṭarkaḷ aṟiyāta oṉṟākum.

ciṟṟiṉpac cirōṉmaṇi nūl:-āṇ peṇṇiruvaril malaṭar iṉṉār eṉa aṟiyum tantiram moccai viraiyai iraṇṭu pāttiraṅkaḷiṟ pōṭṭu, puruṣaṉuṭaiya ciṟunīrai oru pāttirattilum stirīyiṉuṭaiya ciṟunīrai oru pattirattilum taṉittaṉi viṭṭu vantu 5 tiṉattiṟkup piṟaku yāruṭaiya nīrviṭṭa koṭṭai muḷaikkavillaiyō avarkaḷai malaṭeṉṟu kaṇṭu takunta cikiccai ceyya vēṇṭiyatu. ivvāṟē curaikkoṭip payirilum tiruṣṭāntaṅ kāṇalām. āṉāl oru nīr viṭṭu oru mātam kaḻinta piṉṉartāṉ maṟu nīr viṭṭup pārkka vēṇṭum. vevvēṟu koṭikaḷāṉāl orē kālattil pakṣīkkalām.

itupōṉṟa palappala vakaiyāṉa karuttukkaḷ ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil veḷiyāṉa tamiḻ urainaṭai nūlkaḷiṉ vaḻi namakku viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.

ivai maṭṭumaṉṟic cāṭcikaḷ paṟṟiya ‘ruju cāstira caṅkirakam’, kuṭumpattil makkaḷ vāḻa vēṇṭiya vakai paṟṟiya 'illat tōḻaṉ’ (1845), pōlīs naṭantu koḷḷa vēṇṭiya muṟai paṟṟiya 'pōlīs nipuṇaṉ’ (1894) mutaliya pala nūlkaḷ ovvoru tuṟaikkum eṭuttuk kāṭṭākavum viḷakkaṅkaḷākavum aṉaivarum eḷitil uṇarntu koḷḷum vakaiyilum eḻutap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. āciriyat toḻiliyaṟṟuvōrukku utavi ceyyum vakaiyil pala nūlkaḷum eḻutappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivai aṉaittum makkaḷ eḷitil uṇarntu koḷḷum poruṭṭu urainaṭai nūlkaḷākavē amaintuḷḷaṉa. paḻamoḻi, vēṭikkai, viṭukataikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟaic cārnta urainaṭai nūlkaḷum nāṭṭil ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil ulaviṉa.

kāṅkiras ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭilēyē nāṭṭil tōṉṟiṉamaiyiṉ atu paṟṟik ‘kāṅkiras viṉā-viṭai’yum (1890), āciyāviṉ aikkiyamum (1888) veḷivantaṉa.

pāṭa nūlkaḷ

ivaiyaṉṟi, kallūriyilum paḷḷiyilum kaṟkum māṇavarkaḷukkeṉa eṇṇaṟṟa nūlkaḷ ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil ciṟappākap-piṟpakutiyil-veḷivantuḷṉaṉa. avai pala nūṟṟuk kaṇakkil iruppatāl avaipaṟṟi viḷakkam tēvaiyillai. avaṟṟai veḷiyiṭa aracāṅkattār pala caṭṭatiṭṭaṅkaḷum, vaṟaiyaṟaikaḷum ceytiruntārkaḷ eṉa nammāl avaṟṟiṉ muṉṉuraikaḷiliruntu aṟiyamuṭikiṉṟatu. ‘pāla pāṭam’ mutaliya nūlkaḷai āṟumuka nāvalar mutaliya nallaṟiñarkaḷ eḻuti-aracāṅkap pāṭat tiṭṭattil illaiyāyiṉum-iḷampiḷḷaikaḷ uḷḷattil oḻukkam, paṇpāṭu, camaya uṇarvu mutaliya nalliyalpukaḷ arumpumāṟu ākkappaṇi ceytuḷḷaṉar. avaipaṟṟiyellām virippiṟ perukum. (oru cila nāḷai kāṇalām)

potu makkaḷ paṭittaṟiyat takkatāka aṟiviyal, tāvaram, nilam, maruntu, varalāṟu paṟṟiya nūlkaḷum pala ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil veḷiyāyiṉa. avaṟṟuḷ perumpālāṉa āṅkila nūlkaḷiṉ moḻipeyarppukaḷākavē amaikiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ cila kalappu moḻiyil amaintuḷḷaṉa eṉiṉum oru cila nalla tamiḻil eḻutap peṟṟuḷḷataiyum kāṇamuṭikiṉṟatu. cila paḷḷikūṭa nūlkaḷil pala viḷakkaṅkaḷ mutalil āṅkilattilum, piṟaku tamiḻilum eḻutappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. cila nūlkaḷil poruḷaṭakkam mutaliyaṉavum āṅkilattilēyē . amaintuḷḷaṉa. ceṉṉaip palkalaik kaḻakattār ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil paḷḷi iṟutit tērvukkeṉap (Metriculation Examination) pala nūlakaḷ veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷaḷaṉar. āṇṭutōṟum tamiḻppāṭa nūl oṉṟu pāṭṭumuraiyum kalantu veḷivantatu. urainaṭai eḻutiyavarkaḷ peyar kāṭṭappeṟavillai. kiṭaitta kuṟippukkaḷiliruntu, avai aṉaittum āṅkilattiliruntu-palvēṟu āṅkila nūṟpakutikaḷiliruntu poṟukki eṭukkap peṟṟaṉavāki-oruvarālēyē moḻipeyarkkap peṟṟu nūlākkap peṟṟa nilaiyai aṟiya muṭikiṉṟatu. ivvāṟē pala moḻipeyarppu nūlkaḷum, kaṭṭuraikaḷum tamiḻil urainaṭai vaḷarkka utaviyuḷḷaṉa.

pāṭanūlkaḷaip paṟṟi eṇṇumpōtu attuṟaiyiluḷḷa palvēṟu nūlkaḷum nam niṉaivukku varukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻppāṭa nūlkaḷ maṭṭumaṉṟi, ellāp pāṭaṅkaḷaiyumē tamiḻil collik koṭuttārkaḷ eṉpatu akkālattil uṇṭāyirunta pāṭat tiṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ amaippiliruntu uṇarkiṟōm. nila nūl, varalāṟu mutaliya pāṭaṅkaḷ paṟṟiya nūlkaḷ āṅkila nūlkaḷiṉ moḻipeyarppukaḷākavē amaintaṉa. eṉavē moḻi peyarppu vakaiyil kataikaḷum pāṭanūlkaḷum amaintu urainaṭaiyai vaḷarttaṉa.

viṉaviṭai vakaiyilum makkaḷukku eḷiya vakaiyil uyarnta poruḷkaḷai viḷakkum cila urainaṭai nūlkaḷ ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil uṇṭāyiṉa. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu ‘cārīra viṉaviṭai’ eṉpatu 1866il veḷivantatu. in nūl ceṉṉappaṭṭaṇattu vaittiya uttiyōka vakuppaic cērnta ma. jakannāta nāyuṭu avarkaḷāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭatu. pala uyarnta poruḷkaḷaip paṟṟi eḷiya vakaiyil viṉāviṭuttu viṭai peṟum vakaiyil innūlum itupōṉṟa piṟa vi-aiviṭai nūlkaḷum payaṉ aḷittamaiyaik kāṇkiṉṟāēm. ittakaiya nūlkaḷuḷ perumpālāṉavaṟṟait tamiḻāciriyarkaḷ allāta piṟa tuṟaikaḷil paṇiyāṟṟiyōrē eḻutiyirukkiṉṟaṉar.

āṅkilēyar kalvi peṟuvataṟkāka amaikkappeṟṟa. kallūri oṉṟiṉaippaṟṟi mutalil (The College of Fort St. George) kaṇṭōm. atait tamiḻil ‘ceṉṉaik kalvic caṅkam’ eṉṟa peyariṭṭu aḻaittaṉar. atu 1812il toṭaṅkappeṟṟu, 1854 varai paṇipurintatu. ataṉ vaḻiyākavum pala nalla tamiḻ urainaṭai nūlkaḷ nāṭṭu makkaḷukkākavum-ciṟappāka āṅkila nāṭṭiliruntu iṅku vantu paṇi purintavarkaḷukkākavum acciṭap peṟṟaṉa eṉak kāṇa muṭikiṉṟatu. pañca tantirak katai (1826), katā mañcari (1826), miruti cantirikai (1826), tamiḻ ariccuvaṭi (1827) ākiyavai avaṟṟuḷ cila. 'ceṉṉai centamiḻ urai caṅkam’ eṉavum oru caṅkam iruntu urainaṭaiyai vaḷarttamai kāṇkiṟōm.

taṉ muyaṟci eṉṟa nūl ‘sāmiyuyal smails’ eṉpār āṅkilattil eḻutiyataṉ moḻipeyarppu nūlākat tamiḻil (1882) vantuḷḷatu. nāṭu uṇmaiyil nāṭāka vēṇṭumāṉal, taṉi maṉitaṉum camutāyamum mēṟkoḷḷavēṇṭiya pala uṇmaikaḷum, viḷakkaṅkaḷum itil kāṭṭappeṟukiṉṟaṉa. appaṭiyē ci. viruttup piḷḷai avarkaḷ eḻutiya ‘ērāṇmai nūl’ (1885) eṉpatu nāṭṭiṉ accāṇiyākiya uḻavu paṟṟik kūṟuvatu. ituvum āṅkila moḻipeyarppu nūlēyākum. in nūliṉ muṉṉuraiyil kirantac coṟkaḷait taḻuvāmal āṅkilac coṟkaḷai atikamāka vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷamaikku, moḻippeyarppāḷar kāraṇam kāṭṭukiṟar. ippaṭiyē vāṉamaṇṭalattai viḷakkac cōti cāstiram (1848) oṉṟu yāḻppāṇattil veḷiyāyiṟṟu. vaittiya muṟai paṟṟiyum (1863), varalāṟu, nila nūl paṟṟiyum (1858) urai naṭai nūlkaḷ veḷivantaṉa. ivvāṟu maṉita vāḻviṉ aṭippaṭait tēvaikaḷ aṉaittum paṟṟiya tamiḻ urai naṭai nūlkaḷ veḷivanta kālam ceṉṟa pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭē eṉalām. iṉṟu atikamāka vaḷarntuviṭṭatāka eṇṇum nam kālattaik kāṭṭilum ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil ellāt tuṟaikaḷum vaḷam peṟṟiruntaṉa eṉak kūṟuvēṉ.

itaḻkaḷ

iṉi, iṉṟu nāṭṭiṉ mutukelumpākak karutum pattirikai vaḷarcciyaip paṟṟik kaṇṭu amaiyalām eṉa eṇṇukiṉṟēṉ. kaṭanta nūṟṟaṇṭil nūṟṟukku mēṟpaṭṭa itaḻkaḷ tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟiṟkup pala camayaṅkaḷil aracāṅkam añcal kuṟaippu mutaliya vacatikaḷaic ceytu tantuḷḷatu. iruntum avaṟṟuḷ pala cila āṇṭukaḷē iruntu maṟaintuviṭṭamaiyaik kāṇkiṉṟāēm.

cila itaḻkaḷ makkaḷukku nalla toṇṭāṟṟi vantaṉa. palvēṟu pakutikaḷait taṉṉakattuk koṇṭa pattirikaikaḷ cila. camaya campantamāṉa viḷakkaṅkaḷum maṟuppukaḷum cila pattirikaikaḷ vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa. yāḻppāṇattiliruntu tōṉṟiya utaya tārakai paṟṟiya kuṟippiṉaik kaṇṭu amaiyalām.

utayatārakai 1841: Morning Star (gp36b L;#6b 2–6i 6m (5.6LLI) (Published in the 1st and 3rd Thursday of every month at one shilling a Quarter, payable in advance).

cañcikai 3.⁰⁰⁰8⁰⁰41 ௵ māci ௴ 4 tikati viyāḻakkiḻamai.

kaṭaicip pakkattil uḷḷa kuṟippu:

Printed and Published at the American Mission Press, Jaffna, by Eastman Strong Minor— Edited by Henry Marly and Seth Payson.

pakka eṇkaḷ tamiḻilum, āṅkilattilum toṭar eṇkaḷāka uḷḷaṉa.

ivvitaḻil intu tēcacarittiram, pirapañca nūl (nila nūl),kārikai, vilaṅkiyal, pañcatantirak katai, kalvip poruḷ, putiṉac ceytikaḷ mutaliyaṉa iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. pañcatantirak kataiyum, putiṉac ceytikaḷum āṅkilattilum eḻutappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. cila taṉitta āṅkilak kaṭṭuraikaḷum uḷḷaṉa.

pattirikai paṟṟi ataṉ kuṟippu: utaya tārakai pattirattil kaṟkai, carittiram, potuvāṉa kalvi, payircceykai, aracāṭci mārkkam mutalāṉavai paṟṟiyum piratāṉa putiṉac ceytikaḷaip paṟṟiyum accaṭikkappaṭum. atu tamiḻp pāṣaiyilum iṭaikkiṭaiyē tamiḻum, iṅkilicum kūṭiṉatāyum eṭṭuppuṟa muḷḷatāka nāṉkāy maṭitta tāḷ aḷavil ovvoru mātam mutalām mūṉṟām viyāḻakkiḻamaikaḷil piracittam paṇṇappaṭum.

itaṉ vilai pattiram oṉṟukku 2 peṉcu allatu ⁰6 veḷḷaic calli allatu mūṉṟu mātattiṟkum mūṉṟaik koṭuttiruntāl appattiraṅkaḷiṉ vilai oru ciliṅku.

ivvāṟu ataṉ viḷakkam virintu koṇṭē pōkiṟatu.

itu itaḻ urainaṭaikku oru cāṉṟākum.

tamiḻ nāṭṭu itaḻkaḷum inta amaippilēyē ceṉṟaṉa. cila camaya vaḷarccikkum, cila peṇkaḷ, iḷaiñar pakutikkum, cila tattuvam pōṉṟavaikaḷukkum ciṟappākap payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ oru cilavaṟṟai uṭaṉ tokuttuḷḷēṉ. iṉṉum pala nāṭṭil ulaviṉa eṉpatu tēṟṟam.

nāḷitaḻ:

1. cutēcamittiraṉ (1880)

iṉṉum cila pattirikaikaḷ paṟṟiya kuṟippukaḷum uḷḷaṉa, ivaṟṟuḷ perumpālaṉa kālameṉum kaṭuṅ kāṟṟiṉ etir niṟkamāṭṭāmal talaicaryntu viṭṭaṉa. oru cila, cila āṇṭukaḷ veṟṟimuracu koṭṭi vāḻntaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaiyum iṉṟu kāṇpatu aritāki viṭṭatu allavā? ivaṟṟil cila āṇṭu malarkaḷum veḷiyiṭṭaṉa. 'cutēcamittiraṉ’ tōṉṟiya nāḷtoṭṭu (1880) iṉṟu varai nammiṭaiyē vāḻntu varukiṉṟatu. taṟpōtu (1978) niṟuttappeṟṟuḷḷatu.

ivvāṟu makkaḷoṭu toṭarpu koṇṭuḷḷa palvēṟu tuṟaikaḷilum avar tam vāḻvoṭu vāḻvāy vaḻakkoṭu vaḻakkāyk kēḷviyoṭu kēḷviyāyc colloṭu collāy ellā nilaikaḷilum, ceyal kaḷilum, muṟaikaḷilum tamiḻ urai kalantu avarkaḷai vāḻavaittu, tamiḻ vāḻvai vaḷamuṟac ceytu, tāṉum vaḷarntu vāḻntu inta nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ etirkālattai nōkki 1901 il kāl vaittatu.

itaṉ vēṟu cila pirivutaḷai nāḷai kāṇalām.
